# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [67] [Can SOC Operators Explain their Decisions while Triaging Alarms? A Real-World Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22001)
*Jessica Moosmann,Irdin Pekaric,Giovanni Apruzzese*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述和实地研究发现，虽然安全运营中心(SOC)分析人员能够正确区分真伪告警（83%正确率），但仅有39%的案例能提供正确的理由解释，突显了需要支持决策与解释的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注SOC内部流程及分析人员面临的大量误报挑战，但很少探讨SOC员工是否能够为告警分类决策提供合理的理由解释。回答这一研究问题可以更好地指导未来工作。

Method: 1) 对257篇研究文档进行系统文献综述；2) 与真实SOC合作开展实地研究(n=12)，向SOC员工展示真实告警，让其判断是否为真安全问题并解释决策理由。

Result: 分析人员正确区分真伪告警的准确率为83%，但正确解释决策理由的比例仅为39%。

Conclusion: 亟需开发决策支持系统，不仅帮助SOC分析人员做出正确判断，更要帮助他们理解和阐述判断背后的原因。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers (SOCs) are pivotal in modern enterprises. Tasked to monitor complex network environments constantly under attack, SOCs can be active 24/7 and can include hundreds of operators supported by state-of-the-art technologies. Abundant research has studied the internal processes of SOCs, highlighting their pros and cons, as well as the challenges faced by SOC analysts -- such as dealing with the overwhelming number of false alarms triggered by automated security mechanisms. In this context, we wonder: given that "someone" must triage the alarms, and that such triaging must be grounded on established knowledge or evidence-based reasoning, can SOC employees justify why a certain decision was taken while triaging alarms? Answering such a research question (RQ) can better guide future efforts.
  We hence tackle this RQs. First, via a systematic literature review across 257 research documents, we provide evidence that such RQ received limited attention so far. Then, we partner-up with a real-world SOC and carry out a field study (n=12) with SOC employees. We show them real alarms raised in their SOC, and inquire whether such alarms are indicative of true security problems or not. Then, we ask to explain their decision. We found that while most analysts were able to separate "true from false" alarms (the decision was correct in 83% of the cases), a correct justification was hardly provided (only 39% of the provided explanations reflected the actual root cause). Ultimately, our results highlight the need for decision-support systems that help SOC analysts not only make the right call -- but also understand and articulate why it is right.

</details>


### [68] [Sovereign Agentic Loops: Decoupling AI Reasoning from Execution in Real-World Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22136)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Sovereign Agentic Loops (SAL)控制平面架构，通过结构化意图验证、混淆膜和证据链，在策略层阻止93%的不安全意图、拒绝剩余7%，实现策略绑定执行、身份隔离和确定性重放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体架构直接将随机模型输出传递给执行层，由于无法保证模型在运行时的正确性、上下文感知和对齐性，这种耦合带来了安全风险。

Method: SAL架构包含三个核心组件：1) 控制平面验证模型输出的结构化意图（含理由）；2) 混淆膜限制模型访问身份敏感状态；3) 加密链接的证据链确保可审计性和重放。

Result: 在OpenKedge云基础设施原型中，SAL在策略层阻止93%的不安全意图，通过一致性检查拒绝剩余7%，基准测试中完全防止不安全执行，中位延迟仅增加12.4毫秒。

Conclusion: SAL在可接受延迟开销下，通过解耦模型输出与执行实现了策略绑定执行、身份隔离和确定性重放三大安全属性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly issue API calls that mutate real systems, yet many current architectures pass stochastic model outputs directly to execution layers. We argue that this coupling creates a safety risk because model correctness, context awareness, and alignment cannot be assumed at execution time. We introduce Sovereign Agentic Loops (SAL), a control-plane architecture in which models emit structured intents with justifications, and the control plane validates those intents against true system state and policy before execution. SAL combines an obfuscation membrane, which limits model access to identity-sensitive state, with a cryptographically linked Evidence Chain for auditability and replay. We formalize SAL and show that, under the stated assumptions, it provides policy-bounded execution, identity isolation, and deterministic replay. In an OpenKedge prototype for cloud infrastructure, SAL blocks 93% of unsafe intents at the policy layer, rejects the remaining 7% via consistency checks, prevents unsafe executions in our benchmark, and adds 12.4 ms median latency.

</details>


### [69] [PrivSTRUCT: Untangling Data Purpose Compliance of Privacy Policies in Google Play Store](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22157)
*Bhanuka Silva,Anirban Mahanti,Aruna Seneviratne,Suranga Senevirante*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PrivSTRUCT框架，利用文档结构信息（章节标题）来解耦隐私政策中复杂的数据披露，相比现有方法提取出2倍以上的数据项和目的片段，并在3756个Android应用数据集中发现开发者使用全局定义目的时，第一方收集和第三方共享的目的夸大概率分别高出20.4%和9.7%，且敏感第三方数据流常被模糊归类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将隐私政策视为扁平文本，忽略了文档的逻辑层次结构（如章节标题），导致自动化方法混淆不同的数据实践，尤其在将敏感数据项链接到特定目的时效果不佳。

Method: 提出PrivSTRUCT，一种新颖的系统性编码器-解码器框架，利用开发者定义的章节结构线索来解耦复杂隐私披露。在3756个Android应用的大规模数据集上进行评估，并与现有最优工具PoliGrapher进行对比。

Result: PrivSTRUCT提取的数据项和目的片段数量是PoliGrapher的2倍以上，同时保留了开发者的结构线索。发现第一方收集和第三方共享中，使用全局定义目的比局部范围披露的目的夸大概率分别高20.4%和9.7%。敏感第三方数据流（如共享金融数据进行分析）常被稀释和混淆到通用或不相关类别中。

Conclusion: 当前目的披露领域存在持续失败的问题，开发者利用全局定义目的会显著增加数据目的夸大的概率，且敏感数据流的透明性严重不足。

Abstract: Existing research typically treats privacy policies as flat, uniform text, extracting information without regard for the document's logical hierarchy. Disregard for structural cues of section headings designed to guide the reader, often leads automated methods to entangle distinct data practices, particularly when linking sensitive data items to their specific purposes. To address this, we introduce PrivSTRUCT, a novel and systematic encoder and decoder combined framework that to untangle complex privacy disclosures. Benchmarking against the state-of-the-art tool PoliGrapher reveals that PrivSTRUCT robustly extracts more than x2 the number of data item and purpose excerpts while retaining developer-defined structural cues. By applying PrivSTRUCT to a large-scale dataset of 3,756 Android apps, we uncover a critical transparency gap: the probability of developers overstating a data purpose is 20.4% higher for first-party collection and 9.7% higher for third-party sharing when they rely on globally defined purposes rather than specific, locally scoped disclosures. Alarmingly, we find that sensitive third-party data flows such as sharing financial data for analytics are frequently diluted and entangled into generic or unrelated categories, highlighting a persistent failure in the current purpose disclosure landscape.

</details>


### [70] [FixV2W: Correcting Invalid CVE-CWE Mappings with Knowledge Graph Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22176)
*Sevval Simsek,Varsha Athreya,David Starobinski*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FixV2W是一种利用知识图谱嵌入和时间趋势分析的轻量级方法，用于改进NVD中CVE到CWE的映射准确性，实验表明该方法能显著提升映射质量，尤其是对之前存在无效CWE的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVD等公共数据库中CVE到CWE的映射存在不一致和不完整的问题，这阻碍了自动化分析和漏洞修复，需要一种方法来自动修正和提升映射准确性。

Method: FixV2W结合知识图谱嵌入和纵向趋势分析，系统性地分析历史重映射模式，利用NVD和CWE数据中的层级关系，为属于禁止或 discouraged 类别的漏洞预测更精确的CWE映射。

Result: 在2021年8月至2024年12月的数据集上，FixV2W在Top 10预测中为69%的之前存在无效CWE的被利用漏洞预测出正确的CWE映射；将ML模型的MRR从0.174提升至0.608。

Conclusion: FixV2W是一种有前景的方法，能够有效识别和应对新兴威胁，显著改善现有ML模型在NVD数据上的性能。

Abstract: Accurate mapping between Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) and Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) entries is critical for effective vulnerability management and risk assessment. However, public databases, such as the National Vulnerability Database (NVD), suffer from inconsistent and incomplete CVE to CWE mappings, complicating automated analysis and remediation. We introduce FixV2W, a lightweight approach that leverages knowledge graph embeddings and longitudinal trends to improve mapping accuracy of the NVD. FixV2W systematically analyzes historical remapping patterns and leverages hierarchical relationships within NVD and CWE data to predict more precise CWE mappings for vulnerabilities linked to Prohibited or Discouraged categories. We run extensive experimental evaluation of FixV2W, based on test data set collected between August 2021 and December 2024. Considering the Top 10 ranked predictions, the results show that FixV2W predicts the correct CWE mappings for 69% of exploited vulnerabilities that had invalid CWEs before they were exploited. We also show that FixV2W significantly improves the performance of ML models relying on NVD data. For instance, for a model geared at uncovering unknown CVE-CWE mappings, FixV2W improves the Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR) from 0.174 to 0.608. These results show that FixV2W is a promising approach to identify and thwart emerging threats.

</details>


### [71] [Behavioral Canaries: Auditing Private Retrieved Context Usage in RL Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22191)
*Chaoran Chen,Dayu Yuan,Peter Kairouz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"行为金丝雀"（Behavioral Canaries）机制，通过在偏好数据中注入带触发条件的风格化反馈信号，来检测LLM在强化学习微调（RLFT）中是否违规使用了受保护的上下文数据进行训练。实验表明，在1%注入率下可实现67%检测率（AUROC=0.756），为RLFT管线提供了一种不依赖逐字记忆的新型审计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计方法（如逐字记忆检测、成员推断）在RL训练模型上失效，因为RL主要影响模型的行为风格而非事实记忆。审计者缺乏可靠手段验证提供者是否违规使用受法律保护的检索上下文进行后训练。

Method: 在偏好数据中注入"行为金丝雀"：将文档触发器与奖励特定风格化反馈的偏好配对，诱导潜在触发器条件偏好。若此类数据被用于RLFT训练，模型会表现出可检测的条件性行为分布变化。

Result: 在1%金丝雀注入率下，检测率达到67%，假阳性率10%，AUROC=0.756。验证了该机制能在行为变化而非记忆层面检测训练时数据影响。

Conclusion: 建立了行为金丝雀作为RLFT管线的全新审计机制，使审计者能够检测训练时数据影响，即使这种影响表现为分布性行为变化而非记忆化。

Abstract: In agentic workflows, LLMs frequently process retrieved contexts that are legally protected from further training. However, auditors currently lack a reliable way to verify if a provider has violated the terms of service by incorporating these data into post-training, especially through Reinforcement Learning (RL). While standard auditing relies on verbatim memorization and membership inference, these methods are ineffective for RL-trained models, as RL primarily influences a model's behavioral style rather than the retention of specific facts. To bridge this gap, we introduce Behavioral Canaries, a new auditing mechanism for RLFT pipelines. The framework instruments preference data by pairing document triggers with feedback that rewards a distinctive stylistic response, inducing a latent trigger-conditioned preference if such data are used in training. Empirical results show that these behavioral signals enable detection of unauthorized document-conditioned training, achieving a 67% detection rate at a 10% false-positive rate (AUROC = 0.756) at a 1% canary injection rate. More broadly, our results establish behavioral canaries as a new auditing mechanism for RLFT pipelines, enabling auditors to test for training-time influence even when such influence manifests as distributional behavioral change rather than memorization.

</details>


### [72] [Train in Vain: Functionality-Preserving Poisoning to Prevent Unauthorized Use of Code Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22291)
*Yuan Xiao,Jiaming Wang,Yuchen Chen,Wei Song,Jun Sun,Shiqing Ma,Yanzhou Mu,Juan Zhai,Chunrong Fang,Jin Song Dong,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FunPoison是一个功能保持的代码数据集投毒方法，通过向可执行代码路径注入短小、可编译的弱使用片段，仅污染10%的数据集即可实现有效投毒，同时保持100%的可编译性和功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投毒方法需要全数据集投毒且会破坏代码可编译性，无法有效防御未经授权的代码数据集使用。

Method: FunPoison利用可重用的语句级模板，结合自动修复和保守安全检查确保无副作用；类型感知合成模块抑制静态分析警告增强隐蔽性。

Result: 仅污染10%数据集即可实现有效投毒，保持100%可编译性和功能正确性，对多种高级代码清洗技术具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FunPoison在保持代码功能完整的前提下实现了高效的数据集投毒防御，是对抗未授权代码数据使用的有效方法。

Abstract: The widespread availability of large-scale code datasets has accelerated the development of code large language models (CodeLLMs), raising concerns about unauthorized dataset usage. Dataset poisoning offers a proactive defense by reducing the utility of such unauthorized training. However, existing poisoning methods often require full dataset poisoning and introduce transformations that break code compilability. In this paper, we introduce FunPoison, a functionality-preserving poisoning approach that injects short, compilable weak-use fragments into executed code paths. FunPoison leverages reusable statement-level templates with automatic repair and conservative safety checking to ensure side-effect freedom, while a type-aware synthesis module suppresses static analysis warnings and enhances stealth. Extensive experiments show that FunPoison achieves effective poisoning by contaminating only 10% of the dataset, while maintaining 100% compilability and functional correctness, and remains robust against various advanced code sanitization techniques.

</details>


### [73] [Automation-Exploit: A Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Adaptive Offensive Security with Digital Twin-Based Risk-Mitigated Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22427)
*Biagio Andreucci,Arcangelo Castiglione*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Automation-Exploit，一个全自主多智能体系统框架，用于复杂黑盒场景下的自适应攻防安全。该框架通过跨协议自主外泄可执行文件和上下文情报，弥合侦察与利用之间的抽象鸿沟，引入自适应安全架构缓解DoS风险，并采用条件同构验证处理高危内存破坏漏洞——通过构建跨平台数字孪生进行隔离调试，实现风险缓解的"一次执行"物理目标攻击。在包括未公开零日环境的八种场景中验证了其架构韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前攻防安全领域高度碎片化：企业平台因DoS风险回避内存破坏漏洞、自动漏洞利用生成系统存在语义盲区、LLM智能体面临安全对齐过滤和"实弹"执行风险。亟需一个统一的、能弥合侦察与利用之间抽象鸿沟的全自主框架。

Method: 提出Automation-Exploit多智能体系统框架：(1)跨协议自主外泄可执行文件和上下文情报；(2)自适应安全架构降低DoS风险；(3)针对内存破坏漏洞的条件同构验证——成功外泄目标二进制后动态实例化跨平台数字孪生，通过严格状态同步（libc对齐、运行时文件描述符钩子）在隔离副本中迭代调试破坏性载荷，实现物理目标上的"一次执行"。

Result: 在包含未公开零日环境的八种场景中的实证评估验证了框架的架构韧性，展示了其防止"实弹"崩溃并在实际目标上执行风险缓解攻击的能力。

Conclusion: Automation-Exploit框架成功实现了全自主的、风险缓解的黑盒攻防安全，通过数字孪生隔离调试机制解决了内存破坏漏洞利用中的安全性问题，为自动化漏洞利用提供了可行的统一架构方案。

Abstract: The offensive security landscape is highly fragmented: enterprise platforms avoid memory-corruption vulnerabilities due to Denial of Service (DoS) risks, Automatic Exploit Generation (AEG) systems suffer from semantic blindness, and Large Language Model (LLM) agents face safety alignment filters and "Live Fire" execution hazards. We introduce Automation-Exploit, a fully autonomous Multi-Agent System (MAS) framework designed for adaptive offensive security in complex black-box scenarios. It bridges the abstraction gap between reconnaissance and exploitation by autonomously exfiltrating executables and contextual intelligence across multiple protocols, using this data to fuel both logical and binary attack chains. The framework introduces an adaptive safety architecture to mitigate DoS risks. While it natively resolves logical and web-based vulnerabilities, it employs a conditional isomorphic validation for high-risk memory-corruption flaws: if the target binary is successfully exfiltrated, it dynamically instantiates a cross-platform digital twin. By enforcing strict state synchronization, including libc alignment and runtime file descriptor hooking, potentially destructive payloads are iteratively debugged in an isolated replica. This enables a highly risk-mitigated "one-shot" execution on the physical target. Empirical evaluations across eight scenarios, including undocumented zero-day environments to rule out LLM data contamination, validate the framework's architectural resilience, demonstrating its ability to prevent "live fire" crashes and execute risk-mitigated compromises on actual targets.

</details>


### [74] [Horizontal SCA Attacks on Binary kP Algorithms using Chevallier-Mames Atomic Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22429)
*Gerald Isheanesu Matungamire,Alkistis Aikaterini Sigourou,Gerrit Schrock,Zoya Dyka,Peter Langendoerfer,Ievgen Kabin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明，即使采用Chevallier-Mames原子块模式，椭圆曲线密码系统中的标量乘法kP算法在左右实现中仍易受单迹侧信道攻击，包括带射影坐标随机化的左向右算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆曲线密码系统中标量乘法kP是最频繁的操作，需要抵御单迹侧信道攻击。此前提出的原子性原则和原子块模式旨在提供保护，但本文要验证Chevallier-Mames原子块模式是否真的有效。

Method: 通过软件和硬件实现，对采用Chevallier-Mames原子块模式的二进制右向左和左向右kP算法进行单迹侧信道攻击实验，并测试了带射影坐标随机化的左向右算法。

Result: 证明了采用Chevallier-Mames原子块模式的二进制右向左和左向右kP算法仍然易受单迹SCA攻击，即使左向右算法使用了射影坐标随机化也无法抵御。

Conclusion: Chevallier-Mames原子块模式不足以提供足够的单迹SCA攻击防护，需要更强大的防御措施来保护椭圆曲线密码系统中的标量乘法操作。

Abstract: Scalar multiplication kP is the operation most frequently targeted in Elliptic Curve (EC) cryptosystems. To protect against single-trace Side-Channel Analysis (SCA) attacks, the atomicity principle and various atomic block patterns have been proposed in the past. In this work we use our software and hardware implementations to demonstrate that binary right-to left and left-to-right kP algorithms, when implemented with Chevallier-Mames atomic block patterns, are still vulnerable to single-trace SCA attacks. The vulnerability remains true for the left-to-right kP algorithm with projective coordinate randomization.

</details>


### [75] [SSG: Logit-Balanced Vocabulary Partitioning for LLM Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22438)
*Chenxi Gu,Xiaoning Du,John Grundy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SSG方法，通过重新设计词汇表划分算法（排序-分组-分割），提升低熵场景（如代码生成、数学推理）下KGW水印方案的检测能力。核心思想是将词汇表划分为两个对数概率平衡的子集，从而提高每个token预测的水印强度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KGW水印方案在低熵场景（代码生成、数学推理等）中效果显著下降，原因是词汇表随机划分导致水印强度受限于下一个token的概率分布，在确定性高的生成任务中水印可检测性不足。

Method: 提出SSG（Sort-then-Split by Groups）方法，核心是对词汇表进行对数概率平衡的分组划分，替代原有的随机划分策略，从而提升每个token预测的水印强度下界。

Result: 在代码生成和数学推理数据集上的实验验证了SSG方法的有效性，提高了水印的可检测性。

Conclusion: 通过重新设计词汇表划分算法（从随机划分改为对数概率平衡划分），可以有效提升低熵场景下KGW水印方案的表现，拓宽了水印技术的适用范围。

Abstract: Watermarking has emerged as a promising technique for tracing the authorship of content generated by large language models (LLMs). Among existing approaches, the KGW scheme is particularly attractive due to its versatility, efficiency, and effectiveness in natural language generation. However, KGW's effectiveness degrades significantly under low-entropy settings such as code generation and mathematical reasoning. A crucial step in the KGW method is random vocabulary partitioning, which enables adjustments to token selection based on specific preferences. Our study revealed that the next-token probability distribution plays an critical role in determining how much, or even whether, we can modify token selection and, consequently, the effectiveness of watermarking. We refer to this characteristic, associated with the probability distribution of each token prediction, as \emph{watermark strength.} In cases of random vocabulary partitioning, the lower bound of watermark strength is dictated by the next-token probability distribution. However, we found that, by redesigning the vocabulary partitioning algorithm, we can potentially raise this lower bound. In this paper, we propose SSG (\textbf{S}ort-then-\textbf{S}plit by \textbf{G}roups), a method that partitions the vocabulary into two logit-balanced subsets. This design lifts the lower bound of watermark strength for each token prediction, thereby improving watermark detectability. Experiments on code generation and mathematical reasoning datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SSG.

</details>


### [76] [Information-Theoretic Authenticated PIR: From PIR-RV To APIR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22505)
*Pengzhen Ke,Yuxuan Qin,Liang Feng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了无条件安全的信息论认证私有信息检索（itAPIR）的严格定义，建立了itAPIR与itPIR-RV之间的层次关系，并证明了可将itPIR-RV方案直接升级为itAPIR而无需额外开销，弥合了理论鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认证PIR方案依赖计算复杂性假设，而信息论PIR虽然无需计算假设但无法防御选择性失败攻击。本文旨在构建无条件安全的itAPIR，弥合理论空白。

Method: 提出itAPIR的严格信息论安全定义（统计隐私+完整性）；形式化itAPIR与itPIR-RV的层次关系；证明转换定理：有效的itPIR-RV方案可直接升级为安全的itAPIR且无额外开销。

Result: 建立了itAPIR的严格信息论安全定义；证明了itPIR-RV到itAPIR的转换定理；实现了无额外开销的升级路径。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了APIR领域的理论鸿沟，简化了itAPIR设计，并支持恶意服务器环境下的抗量子PIR实现。

Abstract: Private Information Retrieval (PIR) allows clients to retrieve database entries without leaking retrieval indices, yet malicious servers seriously compromise retrieval correctness. Existing Authenticated PIR (APIR) schemes resist selective-failure attacks but rely on computational hardness assumptions. In contrast, information-theoretic PIR with Result Verification (itPIR-RV) achieves integrity without computational assumptions, yet only provides relaxed query privacy with no defense against selective-failure attacks. This paper focuses on unconditionally secure information-theoretic APIR (itAPIR) constructions. We propose the rigorous information-theoretic security definition for itAPIR with statistical privacy against selective-failure attacks and integrity as core properties, formalize the hierarchical relation between itAPIR and itPIR-RV as a relaxed variant with identical integrity but basic query privacy, and prove a conversion theorem that valid itPIR-RV schemes can be directly upgraded to secure itAPIR with no extra overhead. Our work bridges the theoretical gap, simplifies itAPIR design, and enables quantum-resistant PIR in malicious server environments.

</details>


### [77] [ArmSSL: Adversarial Robust Black-Box Watermarking for Self-Supervised Learning Pre-trained Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22550)
*Yongqi Jiang,Yansong Gao,Boyu Kuang,Chunyi Zhou,Anmin Fu,Liquan Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ArmSSL框架，通过配对差异扩大、潜在表示纠缠和分布对齐等技术，实现在黑盒访问和对抗攻击下仍能有效保护自监督学习编码器知识产权的水印方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSL水印方法无法同时满足两个实际需求：(1) 在窃取的编码器用于下游任务时，提供黑盒访问下的所有权验证能力；(2) 对对抗性水印检测/去除具有鲁棒性，因为水印样本会形成可区分的分布外（OOD）聚类。

Method: (1) 配对差异扩大：强制干净样本与水印样本在特征空间正交，产生可靠的黑盒验证信号；(2) 潜在表示纠缠：将水印表示与干净表示（非源类别）纠缠，避免形成密集水印聚类；(3) 分布对齐：最小化水印与干净表示之间的分布差异，使水印样本伪装为自然分布内数据；(4) 参考引导水印微调：将水印作为小侧任务学习，通过对齐水印编码器与原始编码器在正常数据上的输出来保持主任务效用。

Result: 在5个主流SSL框架和9个基准数据集上的实验以及与SOTA方法的端到端比较表明，ArmSSL实现了优越的所有权验证、可忽略的效用退化，以及对各种对抗性检测和去除的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ArmSSL成功解决了SSL水印中黑盒验证与对抗鲁棒性不能兼得的难题，为自监督学习模型的知识产权保护提供了一种实用且可靠的方案。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) encoders are invaluable intellectual property (IP). However, no existing SSL watermarking for IP protection can concurrently satisfy the following two practical requirements: (1) provide ownership verification capability under black-box suspect model access once the stolen encoders are used in downstream tasks; (2) be robust under adversarial watermark detection or removal, because the watermark samples form a distinguishable out-of-distribution (OOD) cluster. We propose ArmSSL, an SSL watermarking framework that assures black-box verifiability and adversarial robustness while preserving utility. For verification, we introduce paired discrepancy enlargement, enforcing feature-space orthogonality between the clean and its watermark counterpart to produce a reliable verification signal in black-box against the suspect model. For adversarial robustness, ArmSSL integrates latent representation entanglement and distribution alignment to suppress the OOD clustering. The former entangles watermark representations with clean representations (i.e., from non-source-class) to avoid forming a dense cluster of watermark samples, while the latter minimizes the distributional discrepancy between watermark and clean representations, thereby disguising watermark samples as natural in-distribution data. For utility, a reference-guided watermark tuning strategy is designed to allow the watermark to be learned as a small side task without affecting the main task by aligning the watermarked encoder's outputs with those of the original clean encoder on normal data. Extensive experiments across five mainstream SSL frameworks and nine benchmark datasets, along with end-to-end comparisons with SOTAs, demonstrate that ArmSSL achieves superior ownership verification, negligible utility degradation, and strong robustness against various adversarial detection and removal.

</details>


### [78] [Adversarial Co-Evolution of Malware and Detection Models: A Bilevel Optimization Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22569)
*Olha Jurečková,Martin Jureček,Matouš Kozák,Róbert Lórencz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于双层优化的鲁棒防御框架，通过建模攻防双方的对抗共演过程来抵御基于强化学习的对抗性攻击。实验表明，该方法将逃避率从90%降至0-1.89%，并将攻击查询成本提升两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的恶意软件检测器容易受到对抗性攻击，传统防御方法（如单次对抗训练）在面对使用强化学习的自适应攻击者时效果不佳。

Method: 提出基于双层优化的鲁棒防御框架，将防御者与攻击者之间的战略互动显式建模为对抗性共演过程。

Result: 在MAB-malware框架上对Mokes、Strab和DCRat三种恶意软件家族进行评估：标准分类器和基本对抗重训练的逃避率高达90%，而双层优化方法将逃避率降至0-1.89%，并将攻击查询复杂度提升两个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过双层优化对攻防迭代周期进行建模，对于开发能够抵御不断演进的对抗性威胁的弹性恶意软件检测系统至关重要。

Abstract: Machine learning-based malware detectors are increasingly vulnerable to adversarial examples. Traditional defenses, such as one-shot adversarial training, often fail against adaptive attackers who use reinforcement learning to bypass detection. This paper proposes a robust defense framework based on bilevel optimization, explicitly modeling the strategic interaction between a defender and an attacker as an adversarial co-evolutionary process. We evaluate our approach using the MAB-malware framework against three distinct malware families: Mokes, Strab, and DCRat. Our experimental results demonstrate that while standard classifiers and basic adversarial retraining often remain vulnerable, showing evasion rates as high as 90 %, the proposed bilevel optimization approach consistently achieves near-total immunity, reducing evasion rates to 0 - 1.89 %. Furthermore, the iterative framework significantly increases the attacker's query complexity, raising the average cost of successful evasion by up to two orders of magnitude. These findings suggest that modeling the iterative cycle of attack and defense through bilevel optimization is essential for developing resilient malware detection systems capable of withstanding evolving adversarial threats.

</details>


### [79] [Detecting Concept Drift in Evolving Malware Families Using Rule-Based Classifier Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22629)
*Tomáš Kalný,Martin Jureček,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于决策树规则集的结构化概念漂移检测方法，在EMBER2024数据集上使用固定两个月窗口和特征级Pearson相关性作为最可靠的配置，通过多种度量指标（特征重要性、预测一致性、激活稳定性、覆盖率）与准确率下降和数据分布偏移相关联，在六个恶意软件家族上的实验表明该方法与现有基线具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件分类中概念漂移会导致模型性能随时间退化，现有方法缺乏对漂移的结构化理解和量化手段，亟需一种能够从模型表示层面检测和表征概念漂移的方法。

Method: 采用决策树规则集提取结构化模型表示，在时间窗口上训练分类器；通过特征重要性、预测一致性（agreement）、激活稳定性（activation stability）和覆盖率（coverage）四个量化指标度量漂移；将这些指标与准确率下降和数据分布偏移进行相关性分析；在六个恶意软件家族上采用固定间隔和聚类两种窗口方法，在家族vs良性、家族vs家族两种设置下评估，并与RIPPER和Transcendent基线对比。

Result: 固定两个月窗口结合特征级Pearson相关性是最可靠的配置——所有家族对的漂移-准确率相关性均为正值；没有任何单一方法在所有家族对上占优，各方法具有互补性。

Conclusion: 结构化规则集方法能有效检测恶意软件分类中的概念漂移，不同漂移检测指标和窗口策略之间存在互补性，固定时间窗口与特征级相关性组合提供了最稳健的漂移-准确率关联。

Abstract: This work proposes a structural approach to concept drift detection in malware classification using decision tree rulesets. Classifiers are trained across temporal windows on the EMBER2024 dataset, and drift is quantified by comparing extracted rule representations using feature importance, prediction agreement, activation stability, and coverage metrics. These metrics are correlated with both accuracy degradation and data distribution shift as complementary drift indicators. The approach is evaluated across six malware families using fixed-interval and clustering-based windowing in family-vs-benign and family-vs-family settings, and compared against RIPPER and Transcendent baselines. Results show that fixed two-month windowing with feature-level Pearson correlation is the most reliable configuration, being the only one where all family pairs produce positive drift-accuracy correlations. The methods are complementary - no single approach dominates across all pairs.

</details>


### [80] [Adversarial Malware Generation in Linux ELF Binaries via Semantic-Preserving Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22639)
*Lukáš Hrdonka,Martin Jureček*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对Linux ELF格式的恶意软件，提出了一种新的对抗性恶意软件生成器，在MalConv目标分类器上实现了67.74%的规避率，并发现将良性文件中的字符串注入ELF文件任意位置是最有效的攻击方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性恶意软件研究主要集中在Windows PE文件格式上，而对Linux ELF格式的研究相对薄弱。

Method: 开发了一种新的对抗性恶意软件生成器，以MalConv为目标分类器，使用多种指标进行评估，通过向ELF文件中注入良性文件中的字符串来生成对抗样本。

Result: 规避率达到67.74%，目标分类器的置信度平均降低-0.50；最成功的修改是使用良性文件中的字符串作为数据源。

Conclusion: 目标分类器对ELF文件中任意位置的字符串都较为敏感，这为后续生成更有效的对抗样本提供了方向。

Abstract: Malware development and detection have undergone significant changes in recent years as modern concepts, such as machine learning, have been used for both adversarial attacks and defense. Despite intensive research on Windows Portable Executable (PE) files, there is minimal work on Linux Executable and Linkable Format (ELF). In this work, we summarize the academic papers submitted in this field and develop a new adversarial malware generator for the ELF format. Using a variety of metrics, we thoroughly evaluated our generator and achieved an Evasion Rate of 67.74 % while changing the confidence of the malware detector by -0.50 in the mean case for the dataset used. In our approach, we chose MalConv as the target classifier. Using this classifier, we found that the most successful modifications used strings typical of benign files as a data source. We conducted a variety of experiments and concluded that the target classifier appears sensitive to strings at any location within the executable file.

</details>

### [81] [DM$^3$-Nav: Decentralized Multi-Agent Multimodal Multi-Object Semantic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22014)
*Amin Kashiri,Atharva Jamsandekar,Yasin Yazıcıoğlu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: DM$^3$-Nav是一个完全去中心化的多智能体语义导航系统，支持多模态开放词汇目标指定和多目标任务。机器人通过点对点通信协调，无需中央协调器或全局地图，利用隐式任务分配机制减少冗余探索。在数据集和真实环境中验证，性能匹敌甚至超越集中式基线，并消除了单点故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集中式多机器人导航系统存在单点故障问题，且依赖全局状态聚合，限制了可扩展性和鲁棒性。本文旨在设计一个完全去中心化的多智能体语义导航系统，消除对中央协调器的依赖，同时支持开放词汇的多模态目标指定和多目标任务。

Method: 采用完全去中心化架构，机器人间通过adhoc点对点通信交换局部地图、目标状态和导航意图；提出隐式任务分配机制，结合意图广播和距离加权的前沿选择策略，在保持去中心化操作的同时减少冗余探索。

Result: 在HM3DSem场景的HM3Dv0.2和GOAT-Bench数据集上，DM$^3$-Nav匹配或超越集中式和共享地图基线；在真实办公室环境中使用两台移动机器人成功部署，完全依赖机载感知和计算。

Conclusion: 完全去中心化的多机器人语义导航是可行的，能够匹敌集中式方法的性能，同时消除单点故障，提升系统鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Abstract: We present DM$^3$-Nav, a fully decentralized multi-agent semantic navigation system supporting multimodal open-vocabulary goal specification and multi-object missions. In our setting, decentralization implies operation without a central coordinator, global map aggregation, or shared global state at runtime. Robots operate autonomously and coordinate through ad-hoc pairwise communication, exchanging local maps, goal status, and navigation intent without synchronization. An implicit task allocation mechanism combining intent broadcasting and distance-weighted frontier selection reduces redundant exploration while preserving decentralized operation. Evaluations on HM3DSem scenes using the HM3Dv0.2 and GOAT-Bench datasets demonstrate that DM$^3$-Nav matches or exceeds centralized and shared-map baselines while eliminating single points of failure inherent in centralized architectures. Finally, we validate our approach in a real-world office environment using two mobile robots, demonstrating successful deployment relying entirely on onboard sensing and computation. A video of our real-world experiments is available online: https://drive.google.com/file/d/1QiUSCn5rIvtuTUqtuXLPgmt6S8x9-MCZ/view?usp=drive_link

</details>


### [82] [Seeing the Whole Elephant: A Benchmark for Failure Attribution in LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22708)
*Mengzhuo Chen,Junjie Wang,Fangwen Mu,Yawen Wang,Zhe Liu,Huanxiang Feng,Qing Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceElephant基准，用于研究LLM多智能体系统中的故障归因。与传统仅依赖部分可观测轨迹（仅输出）的基准不同，TraceElephant提供完整执行轨迹（包含输入和上下文），更符合实际开发者调试场景。实验表明，完整轨迹相比部分观测可将归因准确率提升高达76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有故障归因基准仅捕获智能体输出（部分可观测轨迹），忽略了开发者实际调试时可用的完整输入和上下文信息。作者认为故障归因应在完全执行可观测性下研究，以对齐真实开发者场景。

Method: 构建TraceElephant基准，包含完整执行轨迹和可复现环境，系统评估不同配置下的故障归因技术，并与部分观测基准进行对比。

Result: 完整轨迹相比部分观测可将归因准确率提升高达76%，证实缺失输入信息会掩盖大量故障原因。

Conclusion: TraceElephant为故障归因研究提供了基础，促进了反映真实调试场景的评估实践，支持更透明的多智能体系统开发。

Abstract: Failure attribution, i.e., identifying the responsible agent and decisive step of a failure, is particularly challenging in LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) due to their natural-language reasoning, nondeterministic outputs, and intricate interaction dynamics. A reliable benchmark is therefore essential to guide and evaluate attribution techniques. Yet existing benchmarks rely on partially observable traces that capture only agent outputs, omitting the inputs and context that developers actually use when debugging. We argue that failure attribution should be studied under full execution observability, aligning with real-world developer-facing scenarios where complete traces, rather than only outputs, are accessible for diagnosis. To this end, we introduce TraceElephant, a benchmark designed for failure attribution with full execution traces and reproducible environments. We then systematically evaluate failure attribution techniques across various configurations. Specifically, full traces improve attribution accuracy by up to 76\% over a partial-observation counterpart, confirming that missing inputs obscure many failure causes. TraceElephant provides a foundation for follow-up failure attribution research, promoting evaluation practices that reflect real-world debugging and supporting the development of more transparent MASs.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [A general optimization solver based on OP-to-MaxSAT reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21961)
*Yuxin Zhao,Han Huang,Zhifeng Hao*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OP-to-MaxSAT的自动归约方法及基于该方法的通用优化求解器GORED，将多种优化问题统一归约为MaxSAT实例并利用最先进的MaxSAT求解器求解。在136个实例、11类优化问题上的实验表明，GORED不仅能成功求解广泛问题，且解的质量与现有方法无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前优化算法大多针对特定问题类型设计，缺乏求解多种优化问题的通用性。为此，本文希望设计一种通用求解方法，能够统一处理多种类型的优化问题。

Method: 提出OP-to-MaxSAT自动归约方法，将多种优化问题在多项式时间内归约为MaxSAT实例，然后使用最先进的MaxSAT求解器求解。基于此构建通用优化求解器GORED。

Result: 在涵盖11类优化问题的136个实例上进行了实验验证。GORED不仅成功求解了广泛的优化问题，且解的质量与现有方法相当，无统计学显著差异。

Conclusion: 通过引入自动归约，本文改变了优化求解器的范式——从为每种问题类型设计专用算法转变为使用单一算法处理多样问题。该范式下，单一算法的进步可推动多个领域优化问题的进展。

Abstract: Optimization problems are fundamental in diverse fields, such as engineering, economics, and scientific computing. However, current algorithms are mostly designed for specific problem types and exhibit limited generality in solving multiple types of optimization problems. To enhance generality, we propose an automated reduction method named OP-to-MaxSAT reduction and a general optimization solver based on OP-to-MaxSAT reduction (GORED). GORED unifies the solving of multiple types of optimization problems by reducing the problems from optimization problems to MaxSAT instances in polynomial time and solving them using the state-of-the-art MaxSAT solver. The generality and solution quality of GORED are validated through experiments on 136 instances across 11 types of optimization problems. Experimental results demonstrate that GORED not only successfully solves a wide range of optimization problems but also yields solutions comparable in quality to those from existing methods, with no statistically significant differences observed. By introducing automated reduction, this work shifts the paradigm of optimization solvers from designing specialized algorithms for each problem type to employing a single algorithm for diverse problems. As a result, advances in this single algorithm can now drive progress in a wide range of optimization problems across various domains.

</details>


### [84] [BLAST: Benchmarking LLMs with ASP-based Structured Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22306)
*Manuel Alejandro Borroto Santana,Erica Coppolillo,Francesco Calimeri,Giuseppe Manco,Simona Perri,Francesco Ricca*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了BLAST——首个专门用于评估大语言模型生成Answer Set Programming (ASP)代码准确性的基准测试方法和数据集，包含两个新型语义指标，并对八个先进LLM在十个图相关ASP问题上的表现进行了实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在各类任务中表现优异，但它们在处理声明式编程范式（如Answer Set Programming）方面的有效性尚未得到充分研究，缺乏专门的评估基准。

Method: 提出了BLAST基准测试框架，包含结构化评估方法和两个新颖的语义指标，用于衡量LLM生成ASP代码的准确性。选取了十个经典的图相关问题作为测试任务，以及八个主流LLM作为评估对象。

Result: 对十个ASP文献中的图相关问题及八个先进LLM进行了实证评估，得到了各模型在ASP代码生成任务上的性能结果。

Conclusion: BLAST为评估LLM在ASP代码生成方面的能力提供了首个专门的基准框架，填补了该领域的评估空白。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance across a broad spectrum of tasks, including natural language understanding, dialogue systems, and code generation. Despite evident progress, less attention has been paid to their effectiveness in handling declarative paradigms such as Answer Set Programming (ASP), to date. In this paper we introduce BLAST: The first dedicated benchmarking methodology and associated dataset for evaluating the accuracy of LLMs in generating ASP code. BLAST provides a structured evaluation framework featuring two novel semantic metrics tailored to ASP code generation. The paper presents the results of an empirical evaluation involving ten well-established graph-related problems from the ASP literature and a diverse set of eight state-of-the-art LLMs.

</details>


### [85] [Probabilistic Abduction in a Fuzzy Logic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22064)
*Tommaso Flaminio,Katsumi Inoue,Daniil Kozhemiachenko*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究如何用概率分布或概率陈述来解释事件概率观察（如"20%时间下雨"），形式化为模糊概率逻辑FP中的溯因问题，并系统研究了该问题的计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率溯因研究多聚焦于寻找最可能解释，但缺乏对概率观察（如"下雨概率20%"）本身进行解释的形式化框架。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过模糊概率逻辑FP形式化定义溯因问题及其解，研究完整语言和析取子句片段下的解识别与存在性问题的计算复杂度，并将经典概率溯因转化为FP框架。

Result: 给出了FP中溯因问题在完整语言及其析取子句片段上的复杂度完整刻画，并建立了经典概率溯因到FP的翻译。

Conclusion: FP为解释概率观察提供了形式化框架，其复杂度分析为实际应用提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study the problem of explaining observations about the probabilities of events, such as "it rains $20\%$ of the time", "rain and snow are equally likely", etc. We explain these statements with a probability distribution or a statement about probabilities of (other) events that are consistent with our knowledge and entail the observation. We formalise this problem in a fuzzy probabilistic logic $\mathsf{FP}$. We define and motivate the notions of abduction problems and their solutions. Our main technical contribution is a comprehensive study of the complexity of solution recognition and existence for a given abduction problem in $\mathsf{FP}$ for the case of full language and its disjunctive-clause fragments. We also obtain a translation of classical probabilistic abduction (finding the most likely explanation of a given event) to $\mathsf{FP}$.

</details>


### [86] [Characterizing LTL Formulas by Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22097)
*Balder ten Cate,Dana Fisman,Roi Ohayon,Patrik Sestic*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性时序逻辑（LTL）公式能否被有限个标注样例唯一表征的问题，考察了有限词、超限词和模式化样例三种类型，给出了不同LTL片段在各类样例下的唯一可表征性分类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LTL公式能否被有限样例唯一确定，这是示例驱动规范、调试和学习时序性质的基础性问题，旨在理解不同样例类型的描述能力边界。

Method: 对有限词、超限词和模式化样例三类示例进行形式化定义；在有限词场景下对基于基础约束的LTL片段进行完整分类；在超限词场景下证明大单调LTL片段可实现有限唯一表征；引入模式化样例（可紧凑表示一族有限词的模式）并分析其表达能力。

Result: 1）完成了有限词下基础约束LTL片段的完全分类；2）超限词允许对大型单调LTL片段进行有限唯一表征；3）模式化样例能在有限设置中实现普通有限样例无法达到的唯一表征结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为不同样例类型在示例驱动规范、调试和学习时序性质中的描述能力提供了基础性理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the extent to which Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) formulas can be uniquely characterized by a finite set of labeled examples. We consider different types of examples, ranging from finite words to transfinite words, as well as schematic examples. In the finite-word setting, we provide a complete classification of basis-restricted LTL fragments that admit such unique characterizations. Next, we show that allowing transfinite words as examples enables finite unique characterizations for large monotone fragments of LTL. Finally, we introduce schematic examples, i.e., patterns that compactly represent a family of finite words, and we show that these enable unique characterization results in the finite setting that were not possible with ordinary finite examples alone. Overall, the work provides a foundational account of the descriptive power of different example types for example-driven specification, debugging, and learning of temporal properties.

</details>


### [87] [Dynamic Planar Graph Isomorphism is in DynFO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22365)
*Samir Datta,Asif Khan,Felix Tschirbs,Nils Vortmeier,Thomas Zeume*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文证明了动态平面图同构问题属于动态描述复杂性类DynFO，即可以用一阶逻辑公式和多项式大小的辅助数据来维护两个动态平面图（边插入和删除操作下）是否同构，从而得到动态常数时间并行算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动态图同构问题，特别是平面图这一重要子类，在边插入和删除操作下是否能保持高效的维护能力，探索其动态描述复杂性。

Method: 使用一阶逻辑（FO）公式配合多项式大小的辅助数据来维护两个动态平面图的同构关系，将问题归入DynFO类。

Result: 动态平面图同构问题属于DynFO类，存在动态常数时间并行算法（使用多项式大小辅助数据）来维护两个动态平面图是否同构。

Conclusion: 动态平面图同构问题可以在描述复杂性框架下高效维护，属于DynFO类。

Abstract: Consider two planar graphs which are subject to edge insertions and deletions. We show that whether the two graphs are isomorphic can be maintained with first-order logic formulas and auxiliary data of polynomial size. This places the dynamic planar graph isomorphism problem into the dynamic descriptive complexity class DynFO. As a consequence, there is a dynamic constant-time parallel algorithm with polynomial-size auxiliary data which maintains whether two dynamic planar graphs are isomorphic.

</details>


### [88] [Reelay: Online Temporal Logic Monitoring Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22384)
*Dogan Ulus*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: Reelay是一个统一的在线时序逻辑监控框架，通过将时序逻辑规范转化为同步数据流系统，支持LTL、MTL、STL等多种时序逻辑及其扩展，实现高效的运行时验证，适用于从资源受限嵌入式系统到自主机器人平台等多种部署场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逻辑形式化方法和工具存在碎片化问题，缺乏统一的计算模型和接口来支持多种时序逻辑（如LTL、MTL、STL）及其扩展（鲁棒性语义、一阶量化等），需要一种统一的框架来桥接表达性形式化规范与高效运行时验证之间的鸿沟。

Method: Reelay将时序逻辑规范转化为可执行的计算图，以同步数据流系统方式运行。该架构支持离散和稠密时间语义，以及delta编码的时序行为以减少带宽受限环境下的带宽占用。实现为纯头文件C++库，提供高级Python接口。

Result: 通过代表性案例研究和性能实验，展示了Reelay在多种部署场景（从资源受限嵌入式系统到自主机器人平台）中的实际应用能力，验证了其在高效运行时验证方面的有效性。

Conclusion: Reelay成功桥接了表达性形式化规范与高效运行时验证之间的鸿沟，为信息物理系统的严格分析和运行时验证提供了统一的在线时序逻辑监控框架。

Abstract: We present Reelay, a unified online temporal logic monitoring framework designed for the rigorous analysis and runtime verification of cyber-physical systems. Reelay addresses the fragmentation of existing logical formalisms and tools by providing a single computational model and interface that supports a broad class of temporal logics. These include Linear Temporal Logic (LTL), Metric Temporal Logic (MTL), and Signal Temporal Logic (STL), along with their extensions for robustness semantics and first-order quantification over unbounded categorical data domains. At its core, Reelay translates temporal logic specifications into executable computation graphs operating as synchronous dataflow systems. This architecture ensures an efficient execution mechanism, making the framework ideal for high-frequency data streams regardless of behavior length. Uniquely, the framework supports both discrete and dense-time semantics, as well as delta-encoded temporal behaviors to minimize bandwidth usage in bandwidth-constrained environments. Reelay is implemented as a header-only C++ library with a high-level Python interface, facilitating integration across a wide range of deployment contexts, from resource-constrained embedded systems to autonomous robotic platforms. We demonstrate the practical applicability of the framework through a representative case study and performance experiments, illustrating how Reelay bridges the gap between expressive formal specifications and efficient runtime verification.

</details>


### [89] [The Chase in Lean -- Crafting a Formal Library for Existential Rule Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22531)
*Lukas Gerlach*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文使用Lean定理证明器构建了一个关于存在规则（元组生成依赖）和chase算法的形式化框架，验证了chase结果的通用模型性质，统一了多种chase终止条件，并支持规则中的常量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 存在规则和chase算法在知识表示中非常重要，但文献中定义存在细微差异，且验证正确性和复现证明变得困难，直觉有时具有误导性，因此需要形式化验证框架。

Method: 使用Lean函数式编程语言和交互式定理证明器，构建形式化框架；讨论chase定义中细微差别的设计决策并转化为Lean代码；利用已知结果证明chase结果是通用模型；统一Model-Faithful Acyclicity (MFA)等充分终止条件；添加对规则中常量的支持。

Result: 成功在Lean中构建了存在规则和chase的形式化框架；证明了chase结果是通用模型；形式化了在没有"alternative matches"时chase结果为核心的结果；统一了MFA等终止条件框架；支持了规则中的常量。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了Lean在知识表示形式化验证中的应用价值，为chase算法的理论研究提供了可靠的验证工具，统一了不同终止条件并扩展了常量支持。

Abstract: The chase is a sound, complete, but possibly non-terminating algorithm for reasoning with existential rules (aka. tuple-generating dependencies), a highly expressive knowledge representation language. Although the procedure appears simple, research on theoretical properties and optimization for practical implementations has grown to a point where verifying correctness and reproducing proofs becomes challenging and intuition can sometimes be misleading. Lean is a purely functional programming language and interactive theorem prover whose community actively develops formal libraries for mathematics (Mathlib) and computer science (CSLib). In this work, we present our own endeavor of crafting a Lean framework around existential rules and the chase. We discuss design decisions concerning the nuances of chase definitions commonly found in the literature and show how these translate into Lean. To illustrate the framework's capabilities using known results, we show that the result of a chase is a universal model and outline the formalization for proving that without so-called "alternative matches" it is even a core. Beyond existing literature, we unify sufficient chase termination conditions in the likeness of Model-Faithful Acyclicity (MFA) into a common framework while also adding support for constants in rules.

</details>


### [90] [Reasoning About Probabilities, Actions, and Knowledge in Fuzzy Modal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22459)
*Daniil Kozhemiachenko,Igor Sedlár*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模糊模态逻辑，用于形式化关于动作和知识的概率推理，并在带概率测度的Kripke框架上定义语义，分析了不同表达力片段的可满足性复杂度，找到了多个多项式时间可判定的片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逻辑系统难以同时处理动作、知识和概率这三种要素的形式化推理，尤其是涉及模态公式表达的事件概率陈述（如动作后知识概率的变化、不同动作下事件发生可能性的比较等），亟需一种统一的逻辑框架。

Method: 在Kripke框架上配备概率测度来定义语义；对完整语言及其不同表达力片段，分析在有限分支模型上的可满足性判定复杂度。

Result: 识别出多个片段，其可满足性判定可在多项式时间内完成。

Conclusion: 该模糊模态逻辑能够有效形式化涉及动作、知识和概率的推理，且部分片段具有较低的计算复杂度，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We explore a fuzzy modal logic that can formalise probabilistic reasoning about actions and knowledge. In particular, we deal with contexts involving statements about events expressed via modal formulas, e.g., "after doing $a$, the probability of $A$ knowing that $p$ holds increases / decreases / is equal to $0.25$", "according to $A$, $p$ is equally likely to happen after doing $a$ or $b$", etc. We define the semantics of the logic on Kripke frames equipped with probability measures. We analyse the complexity of deciding the satisfiability of formulas of our logic over finitely branching models, for the full language and its fragments of varying expressivity. In particular, we identify several fragments of our logic where satisfiability is decidable in polynomial time.

</details>


### [91] [On first-order model checking parameterized by the number of variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22493)
*Jan Jedelský*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究一阶（FO）模型检测问题在参数化复杂度下的表现，特别关注以公式中变量数量为参数时，哪些图类允许固定参数可解（FPT）算法。作者在单调图类下给出了完整刻画，并在遗传图类下得到了稍弱的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知FO模型检测问题在参数量词秩时是AW[*]-难的，但以变量数为参数时仅有XP算法。为了寻找更高效的FPT算法，需要识别出哪些受限图类能实现以变量数为参数的FPT可解性。

Method: 作者系统研究了图类C的分类问题：在单调图类（对子图封闭）下，完全刻画了使FO模型检测以变量数为参数时属于FPT的图类；在遗传图类（对诱导子图封闭）下，证明了稍弱的结果（可能是某种近似刻画或必要条件）。

Result: 在单调图类中，完整刻画了哪些图类允许以变量数为参数的FPT算法；在遗传图类中，得到了一个稍弱的分类结果（具体刻画略弱于单调情形）。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了以变量数为参数时FO模型检测问题的可解图类边界，为参数化复杂度理论提供了新的分类结果，并指出了单调与遗传图类之间的区别。

Abstract: The first-order (FO) model checking problem asks, given an FO sentence $φ$ and a graph $G$, whether $G$ is a model of $φ$. This problem is known to be $\mathsf{AW[*]}$-hard when parameterized by the quantifier rank of the formula. A classical algorithm decides this problem in XP-time parameterized by the number of variables in the formula.
  Due to $\mathsf{AW[*]}$-hardness, it is natural to ask about the complexity of the problem when restricted to some well-behaved class of graphs. There are many results describing graph classes $\mathcal{C}$ such that the FO model checking problem restricted to $\mathcal{C}$ admits an $\mathsf{FPT}$-time algorithm when parameterized by the quantifier rank of the formula.
  Parameterization by the quantifier rank is significantly more restrictive than parameterization by the number of variables. We investigate the graph classes $\mathcal{C}$ for which the FO model checking problem restricted to $\mathcal{C}$ admits an $\mathsf{FPT}$-time algorithm when parameterized by the number of variables in the formula. We characterize these classes in the monotone setting, and prove a slightly weaker result in the hereditary setting.

</details>


### [92] [DEKL 2.0: Trace-Indexed Knowledge Evolution in Dependent Type Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22530)
*Chen Peng*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: DEKL 2.0 是一个基于依赖类型理论的迹索引知识演化框架，通过迹扩展实现语义上的非单调性，同时保持证明演算在标准结构规则下的单调性。该框架将有限和无限迹作为计算宇宙中的一等对象，将知识解释为有限迹范畴上的预层，并通过不动点支持处理命题级推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识演化框架在处理非单调推理时往往牺牲了证明演算的结构性质，缺乏统一的形式化框架来同时处理可执行迹、类型化证据和知识修正。

Method: 构建依赖类型理论框架，将迹作为一等对象；利用预层范畴解释知识；通过非满射限制映射刻画非单调性；基于自由范畴（由转移系统生成）给出语义解释；建立迹-可达性对应关系并证明完备性。

Result: 证明了证明演算在标准结构规则下保持单调性，而非单调行为语义上来自迹扩展；建立了迹-可达性对应关系的完备性；用非满射限制映射刻画了非单调性的本质。

Conclusion: DEKL 2.0 成功将可执行迹、类型化证据和知识修正统一在一个依赖类型语言中，为迹索引知识演化提供了坚实的范畴论和类型理论基础。

Abstract: DEKL 2.0 is a dependent type-theoretic framework for trace-indexed knowledge evolution. Its central claim is that the proof calculus remains monotone under standard structural rules, while non-monotonic behavior arises semantically from trace extension. Finite and infinite traces are first-class objects in the computational universe; knowledge is interpreted as a presheaf over the finite-trace category; and proposition-level reasoning is handled categorically with fixed-point support. We establish trace--reachability correspondence and completeness, characterize non-monotonicity by non-surjective restriction maps, and present a semantic interpretation based on the free category generated by a transition system. The framework unifies executable traces, typed witnesses, and knowledge revision in one dependent language.

</details>


### [93] [An Undecidability Proof for the Plan Existence Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22736)
*Antonis Achilleos*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文证明，即使认知动作的前提条件模态深度不超过1且没有后置条件，规划存在性问题仍然是不可判定的，解决了该问题此前未被知晓的(不可)判定性状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究认知逻辑中规划存在性问题的判定边界，特别是在受限条件下的可判定性，该问题的(不可)判定性此前未知。

Method: 通过归约或直接证明的方式，分析在模态深度≤1且无后置条件的认知动作下规划存在性问题的计算复杂性。

Result: 证明了即使前提条件模态深度最多为1且无后置条件，规划存在性问题仍然是不可判定的。

Conclusion: 认知规划存在性问题在相当受限的条件下仍然是不可判定的，这为该问题的计算复杂性提供了重要的下界。

Abstract: The plan existence problem asks, given a goal in the form of a formula in modal logic, an initial epistemic state (a pointed Kripke model), and a set of epistemic actions, whether there exists a sequence of actions that can be applied to reach the goal. We prove that even in the case where the preconditions of the epistemic actions have modal depth at most 1, and there are no postconditions, the plan existence problem is undecidable. The (un)decidability of this problem was previously unknown.

</details>

### [94] [Evaluation of the effects of 3GPP-specific beamforming and channel estimation on the 3D EIRP profile of a 5G gNB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22710)
*Armed Tusha,Joshua Roy Palathinkal,Monisha Ghosh*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次基于3GPP Release-18标准评估了5G NR gNB在3D空间中的有效全向辐射功率(EIRP)，揭示了现有码本设计中副瓣干扰、AAS架构依赖性问题，并提出了两种波束调零方法，相比理论分析可实现11 dB功率降低但伴随3.5-4.5 dB SNR损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多基于理论波束调零分析评估5G基站对雷达、卫星等现有业务的干扰，但这些理论分析过于简化。本文旨在基于实际3GPP标准评估gNB在3D空间中的EIRP特性，识别现有码本设计中的问题，并探索有效的干扰 mitigation 方法。

Method: 基于3GPP Release-18 FR-1标准，对gNB在3D空间中的EIRP进行实际评估，而非采用理论波束调零分析。提出两种波束调零方法，并在理想和实际信道估计条件下，对比不同调制方案在10^{-4} BER下的SNR损失。

Result: 1) gNB的干扰不仅来自最差波束方向，还因副瓣而来自多种波束方向；2) 先进天线系统(AAS)架构和天线端口配置对平均3D EIRP有重要影响，且依赖具体实现；3) 提出的两种波束调零方法可实现目标方向11 dB功率降低，但伴随3.5-4.5 dB SNR损失，高于理论分析预期。

Conclusion: 现有3GPP码本设计在3D空间EIRP轮廓上存在需要关注的问题，实际波束调零的SNR损失高于理论预测，表明需要更实际的评估方法以准确评估5G对现有业务的干扰。

Abstract: Spatial domain exploitation through 3D beamforming serves as a critical technology enabler for performance enhancement in the Fifth Generation New Radio (5G NR) specification. This is realized at the gNodeB (gNB) through the integration of massive antenna element arrays that facilitates 3D spatial multiplexing. However, these systems with high-directional transmissions also represent a threat to incumbent services such as radar and satellites. These incumbents already operate in midband spectrum\textemdash{}including the 4.4-4.9 GHz and 7.125-7.4 GHz bands\textemdash{}that are currently being evaluated for future cellular deployments. Here, we present the first work that evaluates the transmitted Effective Isotropic Radiated Power (EIRP) of a gNB in 3D space, using the 3GPP Release-18 standard for FR-1 instead of theoretical analyses of beam nulling, which can be simplistic. We shed light on the problems requiring attention with the EIRP profile in 3D space for existing codebook designs predefined in 3GPP: i) interference from a gNB does not depend only on the worst-case beamforming direction, but on a variety of beamforming directions due to side-lobes; ii) advanced antenna systems (AAS) architecture and antenna port configurations play a crucial role in average 3D EIRP, which are implementation dependent, and iii) we introduce two beam nulling methods, which achieve a 11 dB power reduction toward a target direction, with 3.5-4.5 dB SNR loss in UE link performance at a 10^{-4} bit error rate (BER) across modulation schemes under ideal and practical channel estimation, a higher loss compared to predictions from theoretical analyses.

</details>

### [95] [Implementation and Privacy Guarantees for Scalable Keyword Search on SOLID-based Decentralized Data with Granular Visibility Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22100)
*Mohamed Ragab,Faria Ferooz,Mohammad Bahrani,Helen Oliver,Thanassis Tiropanis,Alexandra Poulovassilis,Adriane Chapman,George Roussos*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: ESPRESSO是一个面向Solid个人数据生态的去中心化关键词搜索框架，通过WebID作用域索引和隐私感知元数据实现跨Pod的可扩展搜索，本文进一步提出了正式威胁模型，分析索引和元数据生成、聚合和使用中的安全与隐私风险，并给出设计原则来限制元数据暴露和缓解未授权推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Solid等去中心化个人数据生态中，数据分布在众多Pod中且受用户特定访问约束，导致搜索变得困难；现有方案缺乏对索引和元数据泄露风险的系统性分析。

Method: 构建WebID作用域索引和隐私感知元数据，实现高效的源选择和跨服务器排序；引入正式威胁模型分析索引和元数据全生命周期的安全与隐私风险。

Result: 识别了元数据意外泄露和 adversaries 推断敏感信息等风险；提出了限制元数据暴露并缓解未授权推理的关键设计原则。

Conclusion: 该威胁模型为评估隐私保护的去中心化搜索奠定了基础，并为设计具有更强隐私保障的系统提供了指导。

Abstract: In decentralized personal data ecosystems grounded in architectures such as Solid, users retain sovereignty over their data via personal online data stores (pods), hosted on Solid-compliant server infrastructures. In such environments, data remains under the control of pod owners, which complicates search due to distribution across numerous pods and user-specific access constraints. ESPRESSO is a decentralized framework for scalable keyword-based search across distributed Solid pods under user-defined visibility policies. It addresses key challenges of decentralized search by constructing WebID-scoped indexes within pods and employing privacy-aware metadata to enable efficient source selection and ranking across servers. This paper further introduces a formal threat model for ESPRESSO, analysing the security and privacy risks associated with the generation, aggregation, and use of indexes and metadata. These risks include unintended metadata leakage and the potential for adversaries to infer sensitive information about data that resides within personal data stores. The analysis identifies key design principles that limit metadata exposure while mitigating unauthorized inference. The proposed threat model provides a foundation for evaluating privacy-preserving decentralized search and informs the design of systems with stronger privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [96] [MCI: A Maximal Clique Index for Efficient Arbitrary-Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22171)
*Xiaowei Ye,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang,Kaiwen Xue,Daiyin Wang,Xubin Li*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出最大团索引（MCI），一种基于团结构的图索引，用于带任意过滤谓词的近似最近邻搜索（AFANNS）。通过最大团覆盖和局部邻域图几何稠密化两种技术，实现高压缩、高连通性的索引构建，在10个数据集上显著超越现有方法，QPS最高提升一个数量级，且存储开销大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AFANNS方法在存储和计算开销上存在严重瓶颈，亟需一种既能保持高效搜索性能又能显著降低资源消耗的索引方案。

Method: 提出两种核心技术：(1) 最大团覆盖（MCC），利用高维空间的几何传递性将稠密邻域编码为最大团，实现高压缩与高连通性；(2) 局部邻域图几何稠密化，从稀疏初始NNG出发，渐进增大距离阈值局部稠密化结构，恢复全局连通性。索引采用无锁并行构建，并通过多种子策略处理谓词导致的子图碎片化。

Result: 在10个数据集上，MCI在高召回率下QPS比SOTA方法提升最高一个数量级，同时存储空间大幅减小；在范围/关键词过滤任务上也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: MCI通过团结构近似稠密NNG，在AFANNS任务上实现了搜索效率与存储开销的显著平衡，展现出鲁棒的通用性能。

Abstract: Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search with arbitrary filtering predicates (AFANNS) is essential for modern data applications, yet existing methods often incur substantial storage and computational costs. In this work, we introduce the Maximal Clique Index (\mci), a novel graph-based index designed for robust and efficient AFANNS. The core idea of \mci is to approximate a dense Nearest Neighbor Graph (NNG) through a compact, clique-based representation. We propose two key techniques: (1) Maximal Clique Cover (\mcc), which exploits the geometric transitivity of high-dimensional spaces to encode dense neighborhoods as maximal cliques, achieving an index with high compression and connectivity; and (2) Local Neighborhood Graph Geometric Densification, a strategy that constructs an index approximating a large NNG from a sparse initial NNG, recovers global connectivity by progressively increasing distance thresholds to locally densify the structure. The index is built in a lock-free parallel manner for scalability and queried via a carefully-designed multi-seed strategy to handle fragmented predicate-induced subgraphs. Extensive experiments on 10 datasets show that \mci significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods by up to one order of magnitude in QPS at high recall while using substantially smaller space, and remains competitive even on range/keyword filtering tasks, demonstrating robust general-purpose performance.

</details>


### [97] [A Model-Driven Approach to Database Migration with a Unified Data Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22415)
*María J. Ortín,José R. Hoyos,Jesus García-Molina*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于U-Schema统一数据模型的通用数据库迁移方法，通过中间表示减少异构数据模型间的转换复杂度，并在关系型到文档型数据库的迁移实验中验证了其模式保持性、语义完整性和性能可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库迁移方法通常针对特定的源-目标组合设计，在多模型环境中适用性有限，缺乏支持异构数据模型间通用迁移的统一框架。

Method: 提出基于U-Schema统一数据模型的通用迁移方法，以U-Schema作为中间表示；定义各数据模型到U-Schema的映射，减少所需转换数量；在模式转换过程中生成追踪信息，用于后续解耦的数据迁移。

Result: 实验表明：模式级验证中实现了高结构保持性；数据级验证中语义保持良好，支持连接、聚合、嵌套结构等多种访问模式；性能分析表明方法对递增数据集具有可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法在关系型到文档型迁移场景中得到具体验证，但设计上支持多数据模型共存，提供了一种通用的异构数据库迁移框架。

Abstract: Database migration is a key task in software modernization, increasingly involving transformations across heterogeneous data models such as relational and NoSQL systems. Existing approaches are typically designed for specific source-target combinations, which limits their applicability in multi-model environments.
  This paper proposes a generic database migration approach based on the U-Schema unified data model, which acts as a pivot representation. By defining mappings between each data model and U-Schema, the approach reduces the number of required transformations and enables schema conversion across heterogeneous paradigms. Trace information is generated during schema transformation to capture correspondences between source and target elements, and is subsequently used to guide data migration in a decoupled manner.
  The approach has been implemented and evaluated through experiments covering schema-level validation, data-level semantic preservation, and performance analysis. The results show that the migration pipeline achieves high structural preservation under round-trip reconstruction, produces document schemas consistent with the intended design decisions, and preserves query behavior across a variety of access patterns, including joins, aggregations, and nested structures. Performance results demonstrate the feasibility of the approach for datasets of increasing size.
  The evaluation focuses on relational-to-document migration using both synthetic datasets and the Northwind benchmark. While this scenario provides a concrete instantiation, the approach is designed to support multiple data models within a unified framework.

</details>


### [98] [How Hard is it to Decide if a Fact is Relevant to a Query?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22422)
*Meghyn Bienvenu,Diego Figueira,Pierre Lafourcade*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据库查询中事实相关性判定问题的复杂度，发现自连接是导致该问题比查询评估更困难的关键因素，并通过限制自连接或交互宽度使相关性问题的复杂度降低到与查询评估相同水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给定数据库D、布尔合取查询q和事实f，判断f是否属于D中使q成立的最小事实子集的问题（即查询相关性）对查询答案解释至关重要，但其组合复杂度尚未被系统研究，特别是哪些限制条件能降低复杂度尚不明确。

Method: 通过复杂度分析，证明自连接是导致相关性判定比查询评估更困难（Σ^p_2-完全）的根源；进一步证明禁止或限制自连接可以使相关性问题与查询评估具有相同复杂度（NP或LogCFL）；在本体查询场景中，通过引入"交互宽度"概念（泛化自连接宽度和"无交互"条件）并加以限制，得到类似结论。

Result: 证明了对于CQ，自连接是导致相关性判定为Σ^p_2-完全的关键；限制自连接后，相关性复杂度降至NP（无结构限制）或LogCFL（有界超树宽度类）；对含DL-Lite_R本体的查询，限制交互宽度可使相关性复杂度不高于查询回答复杂度。

Conclusion: 明确了导致相关性判定比查询评估更困难的根本因素（自连接/交互宽度），识别出可高效计算相关性的自然查询类，为查询答案解释提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We consider the following fundamental problem: given a database D, Boolean conjunctive query (CQ) q, and fact f in D, decide whether f is relevant to q wrt. D, i.e., does f belong to a minimal subset S of D such that S |= q. Despite being of central importance to query answer explanation, the combined complexity of deciding query relevance has not been studied in detail, leaving open what makes this problem hard, and which restrictions can yield lower complexity. Relevance has already been shown to be harder than query evaluation: namely, $Σ^p_2$-complete for CQs, even over a binary signature. We further observe that NP-hardness applies already to (acyclic) chain CQs. Our work identifies self-joins (multiple atoms with the same relation) as the culprit. Indeed, we prove that if we forbid or bound the occurrence of self-joins, then relevance has the same complexity as query evaluation, namely, NP (without structural restrictions) and LogCFL (for bounded hypertreewidth classes). In the ontology setting, we establish an analogous result for ontology-mediated queries consisting of a CQ and DL-Lite_R ontology, namely that relevance is no harder than query answering provided that we bound the interaction width (which generalizes both self-join width and a recently introduced 'interaction-free' condition). Our results thus pinpoint what makes relevance harder than query evaluation and identify natural classes of queries which admit efficient relevance computation.

</details>


### [99] [It's Time to Standardize RDF Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22619)
*Pieter Colpaert,Piotr Sowinski*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出RDF消息（RDF Message）概念，将RDF数据集视为原子性的通信单元，并在此基础上定义RDF消息流和RDF消息日志，以解决RDF系统在事件驱动和流式场景中缺乏消息边界标准的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RDF语义和工具在事件驱动和流式场景中缺乏统一的、可互操作的消息边界定义，开发者依赖非标准技术、传输层假设或启发式方法，导致互操作性问题和效率低下。

Method: 提出RDF消息概念，将RDF Dataset视为原子性的单次通信行为；在此基础上定义RDF消息流和RDF消息日志；进一步提出RDF消息概要（RDF Message Profiles），如Linked Data Event Streams或ActivityStreams，用于描述分页、消息结构、排序和保留策略。

Result: 使得消息边界在序列化、传输和存储系统中显式化，支持增量消费和可重现回放，适用于IoT观测、归档RDF流、纳米出版物、SPARQL CONSTRUCT结果处理等场景。

Conclusion: 该提案为RDF流处理提供了标准化基础，目前正在W3C RDF流处理社区组中寻求更广泛的支持和反馈。

Abstract: RDF-based systems increasingly operate in event-driven and streaming settings, where producers and consumers exchange data as discrete units of communication rather than as freely mergeable RDF statements. As existing RDF semantics and tooling do not provide an interoperable notion of what statements belong together as one message, developers often rely on out-of-standard techniques, transport-level assumptions, or heuristics, leading to interoperability problems and inefficiencies. We propose the concept of an RDF Message as an RDF Dataset intended to be interpreted atomically as a single communicative act, laying the foundation for defining RDF Message Streams and RDF Message Logs. The proposal makes message boundaries explicit across serializations, transport, and storage systems, which in turn enables incremental consumption and reproducible replay in use cases such as IoT observations, archived RDF streams, nanopublications, or processing SPARQL CONSTRUCT results. Building on this, RDF Message Profiles, such as Linked Data Event Streams or ActivityStreams, then provide the terms for describing pagination, message structure, ordering, or retention policies. As part of the W3C Community Group on RDF Stream Processing, we are now seeking broader support and comments on the proposal from the Semantic Web community.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>
