### [100] [Robust Localization for Autonomous Vehicles in Highway Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22040)
*Daqian Cheng,Xuchu Ding,Yujia Wu,Xiang Zhang,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对高速公路自动驾驶定位难题，提出了一套鲁棒定位系统，通过解耦3D几何结构与2D道路纹理的双似然LiDAR前端、控制增强EKF、自动离线建图等创新设计，在高速场景下显著优于Apollo和Autoware，并开源了163公里数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速公路自动驾驶定位相比城市道路研究不足，现有方法直接迁移到高速场景会性能退化，面临信息同质化环境变化、严重遮挡、GNSS信号退化及下游任务对精度和延迟的严苛要求等挑战。

Method: 1) 双似然LiDAR前端：解耦3D几何结构与2D道路纹理线索，分别处理环境变化；2) Control-EKF：利用转向和加速命令减少定位延迟，改善闭环行为；3) 自动离线建图与真值管线：高频更新地图保证定位性能。

Result: 在自采数据集（含城市道路和高速公路，共163公里）上，系统在城市场景与Apollo和Autoware性能相当，但在挑战性高速场景下鲁棒性显著更优，且已通过超一百万公里路测验证。

Conclusion: 解耦几何与纹理线索的双似然定位框架能有效应对高速公路的信息同质化挑战，结合控制信号增强的滤波器和高频地图更新，为高速自动驾驶定位提供了实用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Localization for autonomous vehicles on highways remains under-explored compared to urban roads, and state-of-the-art methods for urban scenes degrade when directly applied to highways. We identify key challenges including environment changes under information homogeneity, heavy occlusion, degraded GNSS signals, and stringent downstream requirements on accuracy and latency. We propose a robust localization system to address highway challenges, which uses a dual-likelihood LiDAR front end that decouples 3D geometric structures and 2D road-texture cues to handle environment changes; a Control-EKF further leverages steering and acceleration commands to reduce lag and improve closed-loop behavior. An automated offline mapping and ground-truth pipeline keep maps fresh at high cadence for optimal localization performance. To catalyze progress, we release a public dataset covering both urban roads and highways while focusing on representative challenging highway clips, totaling 163 km; benchmarking is standardized using product-oriented accuracy metrics and certified ground truth. Compared to Apollo and Autoware, our system performs similarly on urban roads but shows superior robustness on challenging highway scenarios. The system has been validated by more than one million kilometers of road testing.

</details>


### [101] [SNGR: Selective Non-Gaussian Refinement for Ambiguous SLAM Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22065)
*Anushka Kulkarni,Sarthak Dubey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SNGR是一个SLAM框架，通过在iSAM2基础上对高斯近似可能失败的窗口进行定向嵌套采样，实现了对错误数据关联下range-only SLAM的高精度故障检测和一致的局部似然改进，同时降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准高斯近似在SLAM中（如错误数据关联、多模态场景）会失效，导致推理精度下降。现有非高斯方法计算成本过高，亟需一种既能保持计算效率又能处理非高斯性的选择性细化方法。

Method: 在iSAM2框架上增加选择性非高斯细化模块：利用联合边际协方差的条件数检测高斯近似可能失败的窗口，对检测到的区域使用全非线性因子图似然进行嵌套采样细化，并引入门控机制以避免多模态情况下的退化问题。

Result: 在具有错误数据关联的range-only SLAM实验中，SNGR实现了高精度的故障检测和一致的局部似然改进，同时相比穷举非高斯推理显著降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: 选择性细化方法在近似SLAM后验中既有潜力也有局限性，能够在控制计算开销的同时有效处理非高斯性，但并非对所有情况均完美适用。

Abstract: We present Selective Non-Gaussian Refinement (SNGR), a SLAM framework that augments iSAM2 with targeted nested sampling on windows where Gaussian approximations are likely to fail. We detect such regions using the condition number of joint marginal covariances and selectively refine them using the full nonlinear factor graph likelihood, with a gating mechanism to avoid degradation in multimodal cases. Experiments on range-only SLAM with wrong data association show that SNGR achieves high-precision failure detection and consistent local likelihood improvements while reducing computational cost relative to exhaustive non-Gaussian inference. These results highlight both the promise and the limitations of selective refinement for approximate SLAM posteriors.

</details>


### [102] [Wiggle and Go! System Identification for Zero-Shot Dynamic Rope Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22102)
*Arthur Jakobsson,Abhinav Mahajan,Karthik Pullalarevu,Krishna Suresh,Yunchao Yao,Yuemin Mao,Bardienus Duisterhof,Shahram Najam Syed,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Wiggle and Go!框架，通过学习仿真先验实现绳索的零样本动态操控，无需真实数据集或迭代尝试。系统先观察绳索运动预测物理参数，再基于优化方法预测目标条件动作，在多种动态任务中实现高精度（3D目标打击平均误差3.55 cm），且任务无关的系统辨识模块支持无缝切换不同操控任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动态操控任务（如抛掷绳索）容错率极低，一次失误可能导致不可接受的延迟或不可恢复的失败。现有方法要么需要大规模真实数据集估计绳索行为，要么依赖迭代试错改进，缺乏高效、零样本的解决方案。

Method: 提出Wiggle and Go!两阶段框架：(1) 系统辨识模块：观察绳索运动，预测描述性物理参数；(2) 优化方法：基于预测的物理参数进行目标条件动作预测，实现真实环境中的零样本执行。系统辨识模块是任务无关的，可无缝切换不同操控任务。

Result: 3D目标打击任务平均精度3.55 cm（对比无系统参数时15.34 cm）；预测绳索与真实绳索的傅里叶频率皮尔逊相关系数达0.95。

Conclusion: 利用学习仿真先验的系统辨识-优化两阶段框架可有效实现零样本动态绳索操控，任务无关的系统辨识模块支持多种操控策略的无缝切换，显著提升目标精度。

Abstract: Many robotic tasks are unforgiving; a single mistake in a dynamic throw can lead to unacceptable delays or unrecoverable failure. To mitigate this, we present a novel approach that leverages learned simulation priors to inform goal-conditioned dynamic manipulation of ropes for efficient and accurate task execution. Related methods for dynamic rope manipulation either require large real-world datasets to estimate rope behavior or the use of iterative improvements on attempts at the task for goal completion. We introduce Wiggle and Go!, a system-identification, two-stage framework that enables zero-shot task rope manipulation. The framework consists of a system identification module that observes rope movement to predict descriptive physical parameters, which then informs an optimization method for goal-conditioned action prediction for the robot to execute zero-shot in the real. Our method achieves strong performance across multiple dynamic manipulation tasks enabled by the same task-agnostic system identification module which offers seamless switching between different manipulation tasks, allowing a single model to support a diverse array of manipulation policies. We achieve a 3.55 cm average accuracy on 3D target striking in real using rope system parameters in comparison to 15.34 cm accuracy when our task model is not system-parameter-informed. We achieve a Pearson correlation coefficient of 0.95 between Fourier frequencies of the predicted and real ropes on an unseen trajectory. Project website please see https://wiggleandgo.github.io/

</details>


### [103] [Dynamic Coupling and Indirect Control of Jointed Robots Rolling Atop A Moving Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22104)
*Hamidreza Moradi,Scott David Kelly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了非对称两连杆机器人在可移动平台上的运动学与动力学模型。通过关节内部驱动产生振荡，可实现类似鱼类游泳的波动式推进；当两个机器人共用一个可平移平台时，动力学耦合影响各自运动；进一步证明，通过控制平台加速度可有效调控单机器人（含非驱动关节）的航向，实现定点环绕或定向持续运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非对称两连杆机器人在滚动平台上通过关节内部驱动产生推进力的机理，以及多机器人耦合动力学行为，并研究通过平台控制来引导机器人运动的可能性。

Method: 建立系统的数学模型，通过数值仿真分析动力学行为；针对单机器人情况，以平台加速度为输入，推导航向跟踪控制律。

Result: 关节振荡可产生波动式推进；双机器人耦合影响各自运动；平台加速度控制可实现对机器人航向的跟踪，进而实现定点环绕或定向持续运动。

Conclusion: 通过平台控制可有效调控非驱动关节机器人的运动行为，为无驱动机器人系统的间接控制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: An asymmetric two-link robot supported atop a flat platform by wheels that roll and pivot freely, but do not slip laterally, will develop forward momentum if the joint between the links is actuated internally. In particular, oscillations in the joint angle will generate undulatory locomotion suggesting fishlike swimming. If two such robots surmount a common platform that's free to translate with its own inertial dynamics, then the individual robots' dynamics will be coupled so that the locomotion of either robot is affected by that of the other. We develop a mathematical model for this system and present simulations demonstrating its behavior. We then consider a single robot with an unactuated joint rolling atop a platform that moves under control, and show that actuation of the platform is sufficient to dictate the robot's behavior. In particular, with the acceleration of the platform as an input, the robot's heading can be made to track a chosen function of time. This is sufficient to guarantee that the robot can be induced to orbit a fixed point on the platform or to locomote persistently in a desired direction.

</details>


### [104] [V-STC: A Time-Efficient Multi-Vehicle Coordinated Trajectory Planning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22196)
*Pengfei Liu,Jialing Zhou,Yuezu Lv,Guanghui Wen,Tingwen Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出可变时间步长时空廊道（V-STC）方法，通过将时空廊道的空间位置和时间步长同时作为优化变量，减少多自动驾驶车辆协同中的时间占用，在保证安全的前提下提升运动时间效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STC方法在协调多辆自动驾驶车辆时存在时间效率不足的问题，需要一种新的规划框架来同时保证安全性和时空利用效率。

Method: 构建优化模型，将时空廊道的空间位置和时间步长均作为决策变量，生成可变时间步长的V-STC，然后基于V-STC为每辆AV独立规划动力学可行的轨迹。

Result: 仿真实验表明，V-STC方法能实现安全的多车协同，相比已有STC方法产生更省时的运动轨迹。

Conclusion: V-STC通过灵活调整时空走廊的配置，有效提升了多车协同的时间效率，为多自动驾驶车辆协调提供了更优的规划方案。

Abstract: Coordinating the motions of multiple autonomous vehicles (AVs) requires planning frameworks that ensure safety while making efficient use of space and time. This paper presents a new approach, termed variable-time-step spatio-temporal corridor (V-STC), that enhances the temporal efficiency of multi-vehicle coordination. An optimization model is formulated to construct a V-STC for each AV, in which both the spatial configuration of the corridor cubes and their time durations are treated as decision variables. By allowing the corridor's spatial position and time step to vary, the constructed V-STC reduces the overall temporal occupancy of each AV while maintaining collision-free separation in the spatio-temporal domain. Based on the generated V-STC, a dynamically feasible trajectory is then planned independently for each AV. Simulation studies demonstrate that the proposed method achieves safe multi-vehicle coordination and yields more time-efficient motion compared with existing STC approaches.

</details>


### [105] [Energy-Efficient Multi-Robot Coverage Path Planning of Non-Convex Regions of Interests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22189)
*Sourav Raxit,Jose Fuentes,Paulo Padrao,Abdullah Al Redwan Newaz,Md Tamjidul Hoque,Mark Kulp,Leonardo Bobadilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大型非凸感兴趣区域（含障碍物和禁飞区）的多机器人覆盖路径规划（MRCPP）框架，通过全局感知的扫描生成、平行路径最小转弯、安全缓冲区、高效mTSP求解器及改进可见性图等方法，显著降低能耗（3%-40%）和计算时间（一个数量级），并在真实无人机和无人艇平台上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最小能量覆盖规划算法使用元启发式牛耕式工作空间分解，即使以最小能量为目标并考虑能耗约束，也无法实现最优能效；且大多数框架仅支持单一类型的机器人平台。

Method: MRCPP框架包括：(1) 全局感知的扫描生成；(2) 创建具有最小转弯的平行扫描路径；(3) 计算安全缓冲区以确保安全转弯净空；(4) 使用高效mTSP求解器平衡工作负载并最小化任务时间；(5) 通过改进的可见性图连接不连续路径段，跟踪航向角并在安全区域内维持过渡。

Result: 在真实AAV和ASV平台上的实验表明，MRCPP相较于现有最优规划器，3机器人团队平均总能耗降低3%-40%，计算时间减少一个数量级，同时保持平衡的工作负载分配和良好的舰队规模可扩展性。

Conclusion: MRCPP框架在能效、计算效率和可扩展性方面均显著优于现有方法，并已开源发布，支持多种机器人平台。

Abstract: This letter presents an energy-efficient multi-robot coverage path planning (MRCPP) framework for large, nonconvex Regions of Interest (ROI) containing obstacles and no-fly zones (NFZ). Existing minimum-energy coverage planning algorithms utilize meta-heuristic boustrophedon workspace decomposition. Therefore, even with minimum energy objectives and energy consumption constraints, they cannot achieve optimal energy efficiency. Moreover, most existing frameworks support only a single type of robotic platform. MRCPP overcomes these limitations by: generating globally-informed swath generation, creating parallel sweeping paths with minimal turns, calculating safety buffers to ensure safe turning clearance, using an efficient mTSP solver to balance workloads and minimize mission time, and connecting disjoint segments via a modified visibility graph that tracks heading angles while maintaining transitions within safe regions. The efficacy of the proposed MRCPP framework is demonstrated through real-world experiments involving autonomous aerial vehicles (AAVs) and autonomous surface vehicles (ASVs). Evaluations demonstrate that the proposed MRCPP consistently outperforms state-of-the-art planners, reducing average total energy consumption by 3\% to 40\% for a team of 3 robots and computation time by an order of magnitude, while maintaining balanced workload distribution and strong scalability across increasing fleet sizes. The MRCPP framework is released as an open-source package and videos of real-world and simulated experiments are available at https://mrc-pp.github.io.

</details>


### [106] [An LLM-Driven Closed-Loop Autonomous Learning Framework for Robots Facing Uncovered Tasks in Open Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22199)
*Hong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向开放环境中机器人未覆盖任务的LLM驱动闭环自主学习框架。该框架通过检索本地方法库、触发LLM辅助的自主学习过程（包括任务分析、模型选择、数据收集和策略组织），并利用自执行与主动观察进行准实时训练，将验证后的结果存入本地方法库实现复用。实验表明，该方法在重复任务自执行场景下将平均执行时间从7.7772s降至6.7779s，LLM调用次数从1.0降至0.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人在开放环境中处理未覆盖任务时，通常需要反复依赖大语言模型，且成功的执行或观察到的外部行为未能自主转化为可复用的本地知识，导致效率低下且对外部LLM依赖度高。

Method: 提出LLM驱动的闭环自主学习框架：1) 检索本地方法库判断是否存在可复用方案；2) 若无，触发LLM作为高层推理组件进行任务分析、候选模型选择、数据采集规划及执行/观察策略组织；3) 机器人通过自执行和主动观察学习，进行准实时训练与调整；4) 将验证结果存入本地方法库实现未来复用，形成闭环。

Result: 在重复任务自执行实验中，平均总执行时间从7.7772s降低至6.7779s，平均每任务LLM调用次数从1.0降至0.2，验证了框架在降低执行时间与LLM依赖方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过闭环自主学习机制，使机器人能够将自执行和观察获得的经验转化为可复用的本地能力，逐步减少对外部LLM的重复依赖，提升了开放环境下任务处理的效率和自主性。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in open environments need the ability to continuously handle tasks that are not covered by predefined local methods. However, existing approaches often rely on repeated large-language-model (LLM) interaction for uncovered tasks, and even successful executions or observed successful external behaviors are not always autonomously transformed into reusable local knowledge. In this paper, we propose an LLM-driven closed-loop autonomous learning framework for robots facing uncovered tasks in open environments. The proposed framework first retrieves the local method library to determine whether a reusable solution already exists for the current task or observed event. If no suitable method is found, it triggers an autonomous learning process in which the LLM serves as a high-level reasoning component for task analysis, candidate model selection, data collection planning, and execution or observation strategy organization. The robot then learns from both self-execution and active observation, performs quasi-real-time training and adjustment, and consolidates the validated result into the local method library for future reuse. Through this recurring closed-loop process, the robot gradually converts both execution-derived and observation-derived experience into reusable local capability while reducing future dependence on repeated external LLM interaction. Results show that the proposed framework reduces execution time and LLM dependence in both repeated-task self-execution and observation-driven settings, for example reducing the average total execution time from 7.7772s to 6.7779s and the average number of LLM calls per task from 1.0 to 0.2 in the repeated-task self-execution experiments.

</details>


### [107] [Learning-augmented robotic automation for real-world manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22235)
*Yunho Kim,Quan Nguyen,Taewhan Kim,Youngjin Heo,Joonho Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了学习增强型机器人自动化系统（LARA），将学习型任务控制器和神经3D安全监控器集成到传统工业流程中。在电动马达生产线上部署后，该系统仅用不到20分钟的真实数据训练，便实现了5小时10分钟的连续运行，生产108个电机，通过率达99.4%，接近人类节拍时间并降低了质量变异，为基于学习的工业自动化提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人虽广泛使用，但大多数操作仍依赖固定的路点脚本，对环境变化鲁棒性差。基于学习的控制方法虽有适应性优势，但尚未证明能在实际生产线上长时间可靠运行并保证安全和质量。因此，作者希望弥合学习方法与工业部署之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出LARA系统，包含两个核心组件：1）学习型任务控制器，利用不到20分钟的真实世界数据训练；2）神经3D安全监控器，用于实时检测安全隐患。该系统被部署在电动马达生产线上，执行可变形线缆插入和焊接任务，且未使用物理围栏。

Result: 系统连续运行5小时10分钟，生产108个电机，产品级质量测试通过率达99.4%；接近人类节拍时间，同时降低了焊点质量和循环时间的变异度。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将学习方法与工业自动化相结合是可行的，为在真实制造环境中推广学习增强型机器人自动化提供了实践路径。

Abstract: Industrial robots are widely used in manufacturing, yet most manipulation still depends on fixed waypoint scripts that are brittle to environmental changes. Learning-based control offers a more adaptive alternative, but it remains unclear whether such methods, still mostly confined to laboratory demonstrations, can sustain hours of reliable operation, deliver consistent quality, and behave safely around people on a live production line. Here we present Learning-Augmented Robotic Automation, a hybrid system that integrates learned task controllers and a neural 3D safety monitor into conventional industrial workflows. We deployed the system on an electric-motor production line to automate deformable cable insertion and soldering under real manufacturing constraints, a step previously performed manually by human workers. With less than 20 min of real-world data per task, the system operated continuously for 5 h 10 min, producing 108 motors without physical fencing and achieving a 99.4% pass rate on product-level quality-control tests. It maintained near-human takt time while reducing variability in solder-joint quality and cycle time. These results establish a practical pathway for extending industrial automation with learning-based methods.

</details>


### [108] [CodeGraphVLP: Code-as-Planner Meets Semantic-Graph State for Non-Markovian Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22238)
*Khoa Vo,Sieu Tran,Taisei Hanyu,Yuki Ikebe,Duy Nguyen,Bui Duy Quoc Nghi,Minh Vu,Anthony Gunderman,Chase Rainwater,Anh Nguyen,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CodeGraphVLP是一个分层框架，通过持久化语义图状态、可执行代码规划器和进度引导的视觉语言提示，使VLA模型能够在非马尔可夫长时域任务中实现可靠的机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型作为短视界策略部署，假设最新观测足以进行动作推理，但在非马尔可夫长时域任务中，任务相关信息可能被遮挡或仅出现在轨迹早期，且杂波和干扰物使细粒度视觉定位变得脆弱。

Method: 提出CodeGraphVLP分层框架：(1) 持久化语义图维护部分可观测下的任务相关实体和关系；(2) 可执行代码规划器在语义图上执行高效的进度检查，输出子任务指令及子任务相关物体；(3) 利用输出构建去杂波观测，聚焦VLA执行器于关键证据。

Result: 在真实世界非马尔可夫任务上，CodeGraphVLP相比强VLA基线和带历史记录的变体显著提升任务完成率，同时相比VLM-in-the-loop规划大幅降低规划延迟。

Conclusion: 通过消融实验确认了各组件的贡献，表明该分层框架能有效解决VLA在长时域操作中的非马尔可夫挑战。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models promise generalist robot manipulation, but are typically trained and deployed as short-horizon policies that assume the latest observation is sufficient for action reasoning. This assumption breaks in non-Markovian long-horizon tasks, where task-relevant evidence can be occluded or appear only earlier in the trajectory, and where clutter and distractors make fine-grained visual grounding brittle. We present CodeGraphVLP, a hierarchical framework that enables reliable long-horizon manipulation by combining a persistent semantic-graph state with an executable code-based planner and progress-guided visual-language prompting. The semantic-graph maintains task-relevant entities and relations under partial observability. The synthesized planner executes over this semantic-graph to perform efficient progress checks and outputs a subtask instruction together with subtask-relevant objects. We use these outputs to construct clutter-suppressed observations that focus the VLA executor on critical evidence. On real-world non-Markovian tasks, CodeGraphVLP improves task completion over strong VLA baselines and history-enabled variants while substantially lowering planning latency compared to VLM-in-the-loop planning. We also conduct extensive ablation studies to confirm the contributions of each component.

</details>


### [109] [Learning Control Policies to Provably Satisfy Hard Affine Constraints for Black-Box Hybrid Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22244)
*Aayushi Shrivastava,Kartik Nagpal,Sairam Jinkala,Jean-Baptiste Bouvier,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种为黑箱混合动力系统学习强化学习策略的方法，通过强制策略在约束边界附近呈仿射且排斥形式，并针对混合系统中的瞬时状态跳变引入第二个排斥仿射区域，从而严格满足安全约束。在约束摆和球拍抛接环境中，该方法相比现有方法在保证安全的同时学习到了更高质量的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱混合动力系统存在瞬时状态跳变且显式非线性动力学未知，现有安全方法（如CBF、可达性分析、安全RL）依赖已知动力学或仅通过奖励塑造来弱化违规，难以严格保证安全约束满足。

Method: 强制RL策略在约束边界附近为仿射且排斥形式，为未知非线性动力学提供闭环安全保证；针对混合系统的瞬时跳变（撞击或重置映射），在跳变前引入第二个排斥仿射区域，防止跳变后状态违反约束；推导了策略满足安全约束的充分条件。

Result: 在约束摆和球拍抛接两个混合动力系统环境中，所提方法相比奖励塑造和学习的CBF方法，在始终满足安全约束的同时学习到了更高质量的策略。

Conclusion: 通过策略结构约束而非依赖动力学知识，可以有效解决黑箱混合动力系统的安全强化学习问题，在保证严格安全的前提下提升策略质量。

Abstract: Ensuring safety for black-box hybrid dynamical systems presents significant challenges due to their instantaneous state jumps and unknown explicit nonlinear dynamics. Existing solutions for strict safety constraint satisfaction, like control barrier functions (CBFs) and reachability analysis, rely on direct knowledge of the dynamics. Similarly, safe reinforcement learning (RL) approaches often rely on known system dynamics or merely discourage safety violations through reward shaping. In this work, we want to learn RL policies which provably satisfy affine state constraints in closed loop for black-box hybrid dynamical systems with affine reset maps. Our key insight is forcing the RL policy to be affine and repulsive near the constraint boundaries for the unknown nonlinear dynamics of the system, providing guarantees that the trajectories will not violate the constraint. We further account for constraint violation due to instantaneous state jumps that occur due to impacts or reset maps in the hybrid system by introducing a second repulsive affine region before the reset that prevents post-reset states from violating the constraint. We derive sufficient conditions under which these policies satisfy safety constraints in closed loop. We also compare our approach with state-of-the-art reward shaping and learned-CBF methods on hybrid dynamical systems like the constrained pendulum and paddle juggler environments. In both scenarios, we show that our methodology learns higher quality policies while always satisfying the safety constraints.

</details>


### [110] [False Feasibility in Variable Impedance MPC for Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22251)
*Vishal Ramesh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了可变阻抗MPC中，将关节刚度作为瞬时决策变量会导致公式化错误——参数可行集Fparam大于物理可实现集Freal。通过引入无量纲参数α=ω_sT（执行器带宽×任务时间尺度），推导了1D跳跃单腿的解析临界阈值α_crit，证明了低于该阈值时参数预测不可实现。数值验证显示偏差随α减小单调增长（log-log R²=0.99）。扩展到平面SLIP动力学，证实质心和触地时间偏差为主要后果，并发现第二阈值α_infeas<α_crit，低于该阈值限制刚度范围也无法修复可实现性。将刚度增广到预测状态可直接消除失配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变阻抗MPC将关节刚度作为瞬时决策变量，但其可行集大于一阶执行器动力学下的物理可实现集，这被识别为公式化错误而非建模近似，需要严格区分参数可行集与可实现集并刻画失配机理。

Method: 定义Fparam与Freal的形式化区分，引入无量纲参数α=ω_sT刻画失配区间；对1D跳跃单腿进行解析推导得到α_crit闭合形式；通过数值验证（10组参数组合，log-log R²=0.99）确认偏差标度律；将机制迁移到平面SLIP动力学验证质心和触地时间偏差；识别第二阈值α_infeas<α_crit；提出状态增广修复方案。

Result: 证明α<α_crit时无有效刚度命令可实现参数预测；数值实验确认偏差随α减小单调增长，标度律准确；平面SLIP中质心和触地时间偏差为主要后果，摩擦效应为三级可观测量；发现α_infeas阈值，低于该阈值限制刚度范围仍不可修复；状态增广可闭合失配。

Conclusion: 可变阻抗MPC的刚度瞬时决策存在根本性的可实现性问题，需通过状态增广（将刚度纳入预测状态）来闭合失配，这对基于可变阻抗的机器人控制设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Variable impedance model predictive control (MPC) formulations that treat joint stiffness as an instantaneous decision variable operate on a feasible set strictly larger than the physically realizable set under first-order actuator dynamics. We identify this as a formulation error rather than a modeling approximation, formalize the distinction between the parameter-based feasible set Fparam and the realizable set Freal, and characterize the regime of mismatch via the dimensionless parameter alpha = omega_sT (actuator bandwidth times task timescale). For the 1D hopping monoped, we prove that below an analytical threshold alpha_crit derived in closed form from task physics, no admissible stiffness command realizes the parameter-based prediction. Numerical validation in 1D shows monotonic deviation growth as alpha decreases, with the predicted scaling holding across ten parameter combinations (log-log R2 = 0.99). Mechanism transfer to planar spring-loaded inverted pendulum dynamics confirms center-of-mass and stance-timing deviation as the primary consequence, with regime-dependent friction effects as a tertiary observable. A second threshold alpha_infeas < alpha_crit establishes a floor below which restricting the admissible stiffness range cannot repair realizability, closing the conservative-tuning objection on structural grounds. Augmenting the prediction state with stiffness closes the mismatch by construction.

</details>


### [111] [A Kinematic Analysis of Palm Degrees of Freedom for Enhancing Thumb Opposability in Robotic Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22283)
*HyoJae Kang,Yeong Jae Park,Hyunmok Jung,Joonho Lee,Dong Il Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过量化分析手掌自由度（DoF）对五指机器人手拇指对掌能力的影响，发现手掌DoF通过重新定位手指基底位置而非单纯延伸可达范围来显著改善对掌性，尤其在无名指和小指上效果显著；但在总DoF受限时，将手指DoF重新分配给手掌会在重叠工作空间扩展与运动冗余之间产生权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究手掌自由度在增强五指机器人手拇指对掌能力中的运动学作用，为机器人手设计提供定量指导。

Method: 构建包含五指（拇指5DoF、其余手指3-4DoF）的手部模型，在相邻手指间引入手掌运动；基于体素化 fingertip 可达区域定义重叠工作空间体积，量化评估拇指-手指交互；比较七种构型（包括增加总DoF及在总DoF不变下从手指向手掌重新分配DoF）。

Result: 手掌DoF显著提升对掌性，尤其对无名指和小指效果明显；其机制是重新定位基底位置而非扩展可达范围；总DoF受限时，向手掌重新分配DoF会在重叠工作空间扩展与运动冗余之间产生权衡。

Conclusion: 手掌DoF与手指DoF在手部运动学中扮演不同角色，应联合考虑设计；本研究提供了不依赖物体或接触模型的定量评估框架，并为机器人手集成手掌运动提供了设计指导。

Abstract: This study investigates the kinematic role of palm degrees of freedom (DoF) in enhancing thumb opposability in a five-finger robotic hand. A hand model consisting of a five DoF thumb and four fingers with three to four DoF is analyzed, where palm motion is introduced between adjacent fingers. To quantitatively evaluate thumb-finger interaction, the overlap workspace volume is defined based on voxelized fingertip reachable regions. Seven cases are considered, including configurations with increased total DoF and configurations in which the total DoF is maintained by redistributing DoF from the fingers to the palm. The results show that palm DoF significantly improves opposability, particularly for the ring and little fingers, by repositioning their base locations rather than simply extending their reachable range. However, when the total DoF is constrained, redistributing DoF to the palm leads to trade-offs between overlap workspace expansion and kinematic redundancy. These findings indicate that palm DoF and finger DoF play distinct roles in hand kinematics and should be considered jointly in design. This study provides a quantitative framework for evaluating palm-induced opposability without relying on object or contact models and offers practical design guidelines for incorporating palm motion in robotic hands.

</details>


### [112] [LeHome: A Simulation Environment for Deformable Object Manipulation in Household Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22363)
*Zeyi Li,Yushi Yang,Shawn Xie,Kyle Xu,Tianxing Chen,Yuran Wang,Zhenhao Shen,Yan Shen,Yue Chen,Wenjun Li,Yukun Zheng,Chaorui Zhang,Siyi Lin,Fei Teng,Hongjun Yang,Ming Chen,Steve Xie,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeHome是一个专为家庭场景中可变形物体操作设计的仿真环境，支持高保真动力学模拟、多种低成本机器人平台，弥补了现有仿真器在可变形物体模拟方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭环境中可变形物体（如衣物、食物）的操控是机器人领域的常见且挑战性任务，但现有仿真器缺乏对可变形物体的可靠支持，难以准确模拟其复杂动力学和多样材料属性。

Method: 开发了LeHome仿真环境，覆盖多种可变形物体类别（服装、食品等），提供高保真动力学和真实交互模拟，支持多种机器人本体，并以低成本机器人为核心关注点。

Result: 实现了对现有仿真器难以准确模拟的可变形物体高保真动力学和真实交互，支持在资源受限硬件上进行家庭任务的端到端评估。

Conclusion: LeHome弥合了真实可变形物体模拟与实用机器人平台之间的差距，为推进家庭机器人研究提供了可扩展的测试平台。

Abstract: Household environments present one of the most common, impactful yet challenging application domains for robotics. Within household scenarios, manipulating deformable objects is particularly difficult, both in simulation and real-world execution, due to varied categories and shapes, complex dynamics, and diverse material properties, as well as the lack of reliable deformable-object support in existing simulations. We introduce LeHome, a comprehensive simulation environment designed for deformable object manipulation in household scenarios. LeHome covers a wide spectrum of deformable objects, such as garments and food items, offering high-fidelity dynamics and realistic interactions that existing simulators struggle to simulate accurately. Moreover, LeHome supports multiple robotic embodiments and emphasizes low-cost robots as a core focus, enabling end-to-end evaluation of household tasks on resource-constrained hardware. By bridging the gap between realistic deformable object simulation and practical robotic platforms, LeHome provides a scalable testbed for advancing household robotics. Webpage: https://lehome-web.github.io/ .

</details>


### [113] [Adaptive vs. Static Robot-to-Human Handover: A Study on Orientation and Approach Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22378)
*Federico Biagi,Dario Onfiani,Simone Silenzi,Cristina Iani,Luigi Biagiotti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于人手姿态和下游任务的动态自适应物体递送框架，相比静态基线方法，显著降低了用户的认知负荷和生理压力，提升了信任度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人-to-人递送策略多为静态开环或仅调整位置，未考虑人类如何抓取物体，导致用户需要主动适应机器人，缺乏流畅性和人体工学。

Method: 集成基于AI的手部姿态估计与运动学约束的平滑轨迹，实时动态调整递送姿态；通过用户研究对比自适应方法与静态基线，采集NASA-TLX、Human-Robot Trust Scale等主观指标及眼动追踪眨眼率等客观生理数据。

Result: 动态对齐方法显著降低了用户的认知负荷和生理压力（眨眼率），同时提高了对机器人可靠性的感知信任度。

Conclusion: 任务感知与姿态感知的系统能够实现流畅、符合人体工学的机器人-人协作，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Robot-to-human handovers often rely on static, open-loop strategies (or, at best, approaches that adapt only the position), which generally do not consider how the object will be grasped by the human, thus requiring the user to adapt. This work presents a novel adaptive framework that dynamically adjusts the object's delivery pose in real time based on the user's hand pose and the intended downstream task. By integrating AI-based hand pose estimation with smooth, kinematically constrained trajectories, the system ensures a safe approach and an optimal handover orientation. A comprehensive user study compares the proposed adaptive approach against a static baseline across multiple tasks, evaluating both subjective metrics (NASA-TLX, Human-Robot Trust Scale) and objective physiological data (blink rate measured via wearable eye-trackers). The results demonstrate that dynamic alignment significantly reduces users' cognitive workload and physiological stress, while increasing perceived trust in the robot's reliability. These findings highlight the potential of task- and pose-aware systems for enabling fluid and ergonomic human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [114] [QDTraj: Exploration of Diverse Trajectory Primitives for Articulated Objects Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22551)
*Mathilde Kappel,Mahdi Khoramshahi,Louis Annabi,Faiz Ben Amar,Stéphane Doncieux*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出QDTraj方法，基于质量-多样性(QD)算法为机器人生成多样且高性能的轨迹基元，使机器人能够操纵多种铰接物体，在仿真和真实环境中均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人在开放环境中执行自主操纵任务仍面临挑战，现有方法缺乏对同一任务多样化解决方案的考虑，导致机器人无法根据实时约束和环境变化选择最优策略。

Method: 基于质量-多样性(Quality-Diversity)算法，利用稀疏奖励探索，自动生成针对铰接物体操纵任务的多样化、高性能的低层轨迹基元。

Result: QDTraj在铰链和滑块激活任务中生成的轨迹多样性至少是其他方法的5倍，在PartNetMobility数据集的30个铰接物体上平均每个任务生成704条不同轨迹。

Conclusion: QDTraj能够有效生成多样化轨迹基元，提升机器人在真实环境中应对实时约束和意外变化的能力，验证了方法的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: Thanks to the latest advances in learning and robotics, domestic robots are beginning to enter homes, aiming to execute household chores autonomously. However, robots still struggle to perform autonomous manipulation tasks in open-ended environments. In this context, this paper presents a method that enables a robot to manipulate a wide spectrum of articulated objects.
  In this paper, we automatically generate different robot low-level trajectory primitives to manipulate given object articulations. A very important point when it comes to generating expert trajectories is to consider the diversity of solutions to achieve the same goal. Indeed, knowing diverse low-level primitives to accomplish the same task enables the robot to choose the optimal solution in its real-world environment, with live constraints and unexpected changes. To do so, we propose a method based on Quality-Diversity algorithms that leverages sparse reward exploration in order to generate a set of diverse and high-performing trajectory primitives for a given manipulation task.
  We validated our method, QDTraj, by generating diverse trajectories in simulation and deploying them in the real world. QDTraj generates at least 5 times more diverse trajectories for both hinge and slider activation tasks, outperforming the other methods we compared against. We assessed the generalization of our method over 30 articulations of the PartNetMobility articulated object dataset, with an average of 704 different trajectories by task. Code is publicly available at: https://kappel.web.isir.upmc.fr/trajectory_primitive_website

</details>


### [115] [RedVLA: Physical Red Teaming for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22591)
*Yuhao Zhang,Borong Zhang,Jiaming Fan,Jiachen Shen,Yishuai Cai,Yaodong Yang,Jiaming Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了RedVLA，首个针对视觉-语言-动作模型物理安全的红队框架，通过风险场景合成与风险放大两阶段流程，在6个代表性模型上实现了最高95.5%的攻击成功率，并基于生成数据构建了SimpleVLA-Guard安全防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在真实世界部署中存在不可预测且不可逆的物理伤害风险，但当前缺乏有效的机制来主动检测这些物理安全风险。

Method: 两阶段流程：(I) 风险场景合成——从良性轨迹中识别关键交互区域，将风险因子定位到该区域以纠缠VLA执行流并引发目标不安全行为；(II) 风险放大——通过基于轨迹特征的梯度自由优化迭代优化风险因子状态，确保跨异构模型的稳定触发。

Result: 在6个代表性VLA模型上实验，RedVLA发现了多样化的不安全行为，在10次优化迭代内攻击成功率高达95.5%。

Conclusion: RedVLA有效检测VLA物理安全风险，生成的对抗数据可用于构建SimpleVLA-Guard轻量级安全防护。

Abstract: The real-world deployment of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remains limited by the risk of unpredictable and irreversible physical harm. However, we currently lack effective mechanisms to proactively detect these physical safety risks before deployment. To address this gap, we propose \textbf{RedVLA}, the first red teaming framework for physical safety in VLA models. We systematically uncover unsafe behaviors through a two-stage process: (I) \textbf{Risk Scenario Synthesis} constructs a valid and task-feasible initial risk scene. Specifically, it identifies critical interaction regions from benign trajectories and positions the risk factor within these regions, aiming to entangle it with the VLA's execution flow and elicit a target unsafe behavior. (II) \textbf{Risk Amplification} ensures stable elicitation across heterogeneous models. It iteratively refines the risk factor state through gradient-free optimization guided by trajectory features. Experiments on six representative VLA models show that RedVLA uncovers diverse unsafe behaviors and achieves the ASR up to 95.5\% within 10 optimization iterations. To mitigate these risks, we further propose SimpleVLA-Guard, a lightweight safety guard built from RedVLA-generated data. Our data, assets, and code are available \href{https://redvla.github.io}{here}.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Causality and Semantic Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22041)
*Anna Zhang,Qinglan Luo,London Bielicke,Eunice Jun,Adam Chlipala*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了将d-分离（d-separation）这一因果图条件形式化验证的方法，借助安全理论中的非干扰（noninterference）语义，证明了d-分离与该语义定义精确等价，并在Rocq证明助手中机械化实现，从而为实验设计的正确性提供了结构语义基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学实验中常因忽略混杂变量而犯错，尽管因果形式化已由Pearl等人提出，但缺乏符合程序语言社区标准（语法分析对语义的可靠性证明）的严格形式化验证。

Method: 聚焦d-分离这一图论条件，借鉴安全理论中的非干扰概念，提出新的语义定义，并在Rocq证明助手中机械证明d-分离与该语义定义恰好等价。

Result: d-分离与基于非干扰的语义定义精确等价，为d-分离提供了独立于概率假设的结构语义基础。

Conclusion: 该定理为实验设计质量的自动化测试提供了理论依据，可对应验证世界建模假设的证伪方法。

Abstract: The design of scientific experiments deserves its own variation of formal verification to catch cases where scientists made important mistakes, such as forgetting to take confounding variables into account. One of the most fundamental underpinnings of science is causality, or what it means for interventions in the world to cause other outcomes, as formalized by computer scientists like Judea Pearl. However, these ideas had not previously been made rigorous to the standards of the programming-languages community, where one expects a (syntactic) program analysis to be proved sound with respect to a natural semantics. In the domain of causality, as the relevant "program analysis," we focus on $d$-separation, a classic condition on graphs that can be used to decide when the design of an experiment controls for sufficiently many confounding variables, even though the reason that this condition works is often unintuitive. Our central result (mechanized in Rocq) is that $d$-separation exactly coincides with a novel semantic definition inspired by noninterference from the theory of security. This characterization provides a structural semantic foundation for $d$-separation and helps explain why the graph-theoretic condition is correct, independently of probabilistic assumptions. For each given automated test on the quality of an experiment design, our theorem justifies an associated method for falsifying the world-modeling hypothesis behind the experiment.

</details>


### [117] [From Monolithic to Compositional: A Compositional Operational Semantics for Crystality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22210)
*Ziyun Xu,Hao Wang,Meng Sun*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文为面向并行EVM的智能合约语言Crystality提出了一种组合式操作语义，将系统分解为引擎组件和全局组件，使并行执行结构显式化，并证明了该语义与原始单块语义在事务级和代码级上的双模拟等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行执行是提升区块链可扩展性的关键途径，但智能合约语言在并行环境下缺乏形式化语义，导致严格的推理困难。

Method: 提出Crystality语言的组合式操作语义，将系统分解为引擎组件和全局组件；利用编码/解码函数建立事务级双模拟定理，以及局部和全局执行的两个代码级双模拟定理。

Result: 证明了组合式语义与原始单块语义在语义上的等价性；能够简洁地证明关键结构性性质，包括局部性、全局隔离性和独立局部步骤的强交换性。

Conclusion: 组合式语义使并行执行结构显式化，为并行EVM环境下的智能合约提供了形式化推理基础。

Abstract: Parallel execution has become a key approach to improving blockchain scalability, but the lack of formal semantics for smart contract languages in such settings makes rigorous reasoning difficult. Crystality is a smart contract language designed for parallel EVMs, supporting scoped state and asynchronous relay across execution engines. This paper introduces a compositional operational semantics for Crystality. Unlike the original monolithic semantics, the new semantics decomposes the system into engine components and a global component, making the structure of parallel execution explicit. The compositional formulation enables simple proofs of key structural properties, including locality, global isolation, and strong commutativity of independent local steps. Furthermore, we prove that the compositional semantics is semantically equivalent to the original one via a transaction-level bisimulation theorem based on encoding and decoding functions between configurations, and two code-level bisimulation theorems for local and global execution.

</details>


### [118] [Ownership Refinement Types for Pointer Arithmetic and Nested Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22361)
*Yusuke Fujiwara,Yusuke Matsushita,Kohei Suenaga,Atsushi Igarashi*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Tanaka等人提出的类型系统，通过泛化所有权概念以支持外层数组索引，从而能够处理嵌套数组（如矩阵）的程序正确性验证，并证明了扩展类型系统的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tanaka等人的类型系统虽能验证数组和指针算术程序的函数正确性，但无法处理嵌套数组（如矩阵），这限制了其在实用数据结构验证上的应用。

Method: 通过泛化所有权概念，使其能够引用外层数组的索引，从而扩展Tanaka等人的类型系统以支持嵌套数组，并证明扩展后类型系统的可靠性。

Result: 实现了一个基于所提类型系统的验证器，并证明其能够验证操纵嵌套数组的程序的正确性，而这些程序是Tanaka等人的系统无法处理的。

Conclusion: 本文成功将支持指针算术和数组的函数正确性验证类型系统拓展至嵌套数组场景，扩大了可验证程序的范围。

Abstract: Tanaka et al. proposed a type system for verifying functional correctness properties of programs that use arrays and pointer arithmetic. Their system extends ConSORT -- a type system combining fractional ownership and refinement types for imperative program verification -- with support for pointer arithmetic. Their idea was to extend fractional ownership so that it can depend on an array index. Their formulation, however, does not handle nested arrays, which are essential for representing practical data structures such as matrices. We extend Tanaka et al.'s type system to support nested arrays by generalizing the notion of ownership to be able to refer to the indices of the outer arrays and prove the soundness of the extended type system. We have implemented a verifier based on the proposed type system and demonstrated that it can verify the correctness of programs that manipulate nested arrays, which were beyond the reach of Tanaka et al.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Structure-Guided Diffusion Model for EEG-Based Visual Cognition Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22649)
*Yongxiang Lian,Yueyang Cang,Pingge Hu,Yuchen He,Li Shi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出结构引导扩散模型(SGDM)，通过结合显式结构信息的扩散模型实现基于EEG的视觉重建，在抽象和自然图像数据集上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG视觉解码方法局限于自然图像和类别表征，难以捕获结构特征且无法区分客观感知与主观认知。

Method: SGDM采用两阶段生成机制：结构监督变分自编码器结合时空EEG编码器，通过对比学习对齐视觉嵌入空间，并利用ControlNet将结构信息集成到扩散模型中引导图像生成。

Result: 在抽象和自然图像数据集上均优于现有方法，重建图像在低层视觉特征和语义表征上保真度更高，泛化能力强。

Conclusion: SGDM能有效捕捉显式结构几何并生成高保真图像，将神经解码扩展到低维或类别输出之外，支持更高自由度的脑机接口意图解码。

Abstract: Objective: Decoding visual information from electroencephalography (EEG) is an important problem in neuroscience and brain-computer interface (BCI) research. Existing methods are largely restricted to natural images and categorical representations, with limited capacity to capture structural features and to differentiate objective perception from subjective cognition. We propose a Structure-Guided Diffusion Model (SGDM) that incorporates explicit structural information for EEG-based visual reconstruction. Approach: SGDM is evaluated on the Kilogram abstract visual object dataset and the THINGS natural image dataset using a two-stage generative mechanism. The framework combines a structurally supervised variational autoencoder with a spatiotemporal EEG encoder aligned to a visual embedding space via contrastive learning. Structural information is integrated into a diffusion model through ControlNet to guide image generation from EEG features. Results: SGDM outperforms existing methods on both abstract and natural image datasets. Reconstructed images achieve higher fidelity in low-level visual features and semantic representations, indicating improved decoding accuracy and strong generalization across diverse visual domains. Spatiotemporal analysis of EEG signals further reveals hierarchical structural encoding patterns, consistent with the neural dynamics of visual cognition. Significance: These findings validate the effectiveness of SGDM in capturing explicit structural geometry and generating images with high fidelity to individual cognitive representations. By enabling decoding of complex visual content from EEG signals, the framework extends neural decoding beyond low-dimensional or categorical outputs. This supports BCIs with increased degrees of freedom for intention decoding and more flexible brain-to-machine communication.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Quantifying Interface Procedure Coupling Risks in Digital Nuclear Control Rooms: An Event Based Human Reliability Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21932)
*Xingyu Xiao,Mingwei Xiao,Hongbo Li,Jingang Liang,Jiejuan Tong,Haitao Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文基于2021-2025年核电站真实运行事件，提出了界面-程序耦合的系统评估方法，发现界面缺陷显著放大程序风险：42.6%的事件涉及界面缺陷，其存在使程序偏离概率翻倍，语义混淆占界面诱导错误的27.3%，并提出了数据驱动的人因可靠性分析工作流和语义对齐框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字化已根本改变核主控室的人机交互方式，但界面如何量化放大程序风险的机制尚不明确，现有研究缺乏基于真实运行事件的系统评估。

Method: 构建了三维标注框架（布局、语义、失配、标记四因素）和四因素界面机制模型，结合机器学习解释和模拟器验证，对2021-2025年核电站真实运行事件进行系统分析。

Result: 42.6%事件涉及界面缺陷；界面缺陷使程序偏离概率翻倍；语义失配和布局诱因是耦合失效的主要驱动因素；语义混淆占界面诱导错误的27.3%。

Conclusion: 提供了数据驱动的HRA工作流用于数字控制室早期脆弱性识别，并提出了界面-程序语义对齐的系统框架以支持风险知情的设计与验证。

Abstract: Digitalization has fundamentally transformed human system interaction in nuclear main control rooms, yet the quantitative mechanisms by which interfaces amplify procedural risks remain insufficiently understood. This study presents a systematic assessment of interface procedure coupling based on real operational events collected from 2021 to 2025 in a modern nuclear power plant. A reusable three dimensional labeling framework and a four factor interface mechanism model are developed to characterize layout, semantic, mismatch, and labeling deficiencies. Results show that interface issues function as a significant risk amplifier. A total of 42.6 percent of events involved interface deficiencies, and their presence more than doubled the likelihood of procedural deviation. Machine learning interpretation further reveals that composite interface procedure coupling, particularly driven by semantic mismatches and layout induced traps, is the dominant contributor to coupled failures. Simulator based validation confirms that semantic confusion accounts for 27.3 percent of interface induced errors, with overall error patterns consistent with historical data. The study provides a data driven HRA workflow for early vulnerability identification in digital control rooms and proposes a systematic framework for interface procedure semantic alignment to support risk informed design and verification.

</details>


### [121] [Not Another EHR: Reimagining Physician Information Needs with Generative AI Technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21933)
*Ruican Zhong,Jiachen Li,Gary Hsieh,David W. McDonald,Selin S. Everett,Alyssa Unell,Jonathan Carlson,Katie Claveau,Noel Codella,Khalil Malik,Scott Mackie,Eduardo Olvera,Scott Saponas,Eric Horvitz,David Rhew,Jim Weinstein,Jacob Gross,Amanda K. Hall*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过半结构化访谈，探讨了利用大语言模型（LLMs）和生成式AI设计动态、自适应界面以减轻电子健康记录（EHRs）带来的认知负担，并提出了支持临床医生工作流程的生成式用户界面设计考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHRs）虽然提高了数据可访问性，但大量复杂数据也给医生带来了认知负担，亟需更智能的交互方式来减轻这一负担。

Method: 通过对微软内部医生进行半结构化访谈，识别数据导航和综合中的关键挑战，刻画诊断工作流程中的信息需求，并分析医生对AI辅助工作的心智模型及其对交互和信任期望的影响。

Result: 识别了医生在数据导航与综合中的关键挑战，刻画了诊断工作流中的信息需求，揭示了医生对AI辅助的心智模型如何影响交互和信任期望。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，提出了面向临床医生工作流程的生成式用户界面设计考量，认为生成式AI可通过动态自适应界面有效支持医生的信息需求。

Abstract: Electronic health records (EHRs) have improved data accessibility but have also introduced cognitive burden for physicians, given the sheer volume and complexity of the data involved. Advances in large language models (LLMs) create new opportunities to rethink how clinicians interact with medical data through dynamic, adaptive interfaces. In this position paper, we explore how generative AI can support physicians' information needs by enabling more dynamic interactions with patient data. Through semi-structured interviews with internal physicians at Microsoft, we identify key challenges in data navigation and synthesis, and characterize clinicians' information needs during diagnostic workflows. We further examine how physicians conceptualize AI can help their work process and how these mental models shape expectations for interaction and trust. Based on these insights, we discuss design considerations for generative user interfaces that support clinician-centered workflows.

</details>


### [122] [Routine Computing: A Systematic Review of Sensing Daily Life Dimensions Towards Human-Centered Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21934)
*Borislav Pavlov,Jiajin Li,Jun Fang,Yuntao Wang,Yuanchun Shi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了“日常行为计算”领域，整合了截至2025年8月的203项研究，提出了包含时间结构、行为交互、认知特性及变异处理的新分类法，并归纳了四大应用领域（无障碍护理、健康习惯促进、自适应上下文支持、大规模群体洞察）及关键挑战（意图识别差距、个性化与泛化矛盾、隐私问题、数据局限），为HCI领域构建了以人为本的日常感知系统设计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类日常行为具有规律性，但计算系统对其理解仍存在困难。该领域此前隐晦且分散，缺乏系统性综述，亟需整合现有研究以推动发展。

Method: 对截至2025年8月的203篇研究进行系统性文献综述，构建新的分类体系，从时间结构、行为交互、认知特性、变异性与偏差处理四个维度对文献进行组织分析。

Result: 提出了日常行为计算领域的新分类法；识别了四大应用领域（无障碍护理、健康习惯促进、自适应上下文支持、大规模群体洞察）；指出了四项持续挑战（低层活动识别与高层意图的差距、个性化与泛化的张力、隐私问题、数据相关限制）。

Conclusion: 为HCI研究人员提供了基础性框架，概述了设计符合伦理、自适应且以人为本的日常感知系统的原则。

Abstract: Human routines structure daily life, yet remain challenging for computational systems to understand. This paper presents the first systematic review of routine computing, a previously implicit but increasingly recognized field that focuses on computationally sensing and modeling human behaviors. It synthesizes 203 studies published up to August 2025. The paper presents a new taxonomy of the literature, focusing on temporal structures, behavioral interactions, cognitive aspects, and how variability and deviations are addressed. The common goals of routine computing extend across four major application domains, including accessibility care, the promotion of healthy habits, adaptive and context-aware support, and large-scale population insights. Persistent challenges that limit the design of truly human-centered systems are identified, including the gap between low-level activity recognition and high-level intent, the tension between personalization and generalization, unresolved privacy concerns, and data-related limitations. By consolidating these findings, this paper provides a foundational framework for HCI researchers, outlining principles for designing ethical, adaptive, and human-centered routine-aware systems.

</details>


### [123] [Comparative Analysis of Human vs. AI-powered Support in VRChat Communities on Discord: User Engagement, Response Dynamics and Interaction Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21963)
*He Zhang,Bumjin Kim,John M. Carroll,Jie Cai*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过在VRChat Discord服务器上对比分析"用户支持"（人类互助）和"AI支持"（聊天机器人）两个渠道，发现用户对两种支持方式存在不同的使用模式和态度。研究结合定量与定性方法，揭示了AI驱动支持相较于传统人工支持的独特优势与局限性，为优化在线社区的支持策略提供了有价值的见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI驱动支持系统与人工支持在在线社区中的差异表现，特别是用户互动模式、参与度和态度方面的区别，以优化在线社区支持策略。

Method: 采用定量与定性相结合的研究方法，在VRChat Discord服务器的"用户支持"和"AI支持"两个渠道中，分析用户参与度、响应动态和互动模式。

Result: 发现用户对AI支持和人工支持存在不同的使用模式和态度，揭示了AI驱动支持相较于传统人工支持的独特优势与局限性。

Conclusion: 研究结果为优化AI与人类支持系统提供了有价值见解，有助于制定更有效的支持策略，构建更具吸引力的在线社区。

Abstract: The integration of AI-driven support systems within online communities has opened new avenues for enhancing user engagement and support efficiency in recent years. This study investigates the differences in user interactions and engagement within two distinct support channels on the VRChat Discord server: "user support," where human users provide assistance to peers, and "AI support," where an AI chatbot addresses user queries. By analyzing user engagement, response dynamics, and interaction patterns across these channels, we uncover different usage patterns and user attitudes toward each approach. Our research employs both quantitative and qualitative methods to explore the trends in the VRChat community when using AI and user support, highlighting the unique advantages and limitations of AI-driven support compared to traditional human assistance. The findings offer valuable insights into optimizing AI and human support systems, aiming to foster more effective support strategies and create more engaging online communities.

</details>


### [124] [Community-Based AI Learning: Redistributing Artificial Intelligence's Epistemic Authority in Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21986)
*Santiago Ojeda-Ramirez,Symone Gyles,Kylie Peppler*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"基于社区的AI学习"框架，通过将AI参与扎根于学习者的生活与社区认识论中，重新定位AI在教育中的权威性，并阐述了三个核心承诺： epistemic微调、权威再分配和情境化辨别，以实现公平的AI教育。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI系统越来越多地介入学习过程，但常被视为权威知识来源，忽视了学习者的社区和本土知识体系。本文旨在解决AI教育中的权威不平等问题，提出将AI学习重新嵌入社区语境。

Method: 采用理论框架构建方法，整合社区驱动学习和建构主义传统，提出三个核心承诺：epistemic微调（校准信任）、权威再分配（凸显社区知识）、情境化辨别（集体判断何时设计、质疑或拒绝AI）。

Result: 提出了"基于社区的AI学习"理论框架，通过三个承诺将批判性AI素养本地化，重新协商AI在教育中的权威地位。

Conclusion: 公平的AI教育必须通过地点、历史和社会语境来协商权威，将学习者的社区认识论置于AI使用的核心位置。

Abstract: As generative AI systems increasingly mediate learning, they are often treated as authoritative sources of knowledge. This perspective paper introduces community-based AI learning as a framework that repositions authority, grounding AI engagement in learners' lived and community-based epistemologies. Drawing from community-driven learning and constructionist traditions, we articulate three commitments: epistemic fine tuning, redistribution of authority, and situated discernment. Together, these processes localize critical AI literacy by calibrating trust, foregrounding community knowledge, and supporting collective judgment about when to design with, interrogate, or reject AI. We argue that equitable AI education requires negotiating authority through place, history, and social context.

</details>


### [125] [Emergent Technology, Emergent Critique: Students and Teachers Developing Critical AI Literacy through Participatory Design around Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21995)
*Santiago Ojeda-Ramirez,Eva Durall Gazulla,Kylie Peppler*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文报告了一项为期五周的参与式设计项目，三名11年级拉丁裔学生和三名高中教师共同协商生成式AI工具在课堂中的使用与教学方式。研究发现三种关键的AI素养实践：集体质疑AI假设、通过互补专长相互学习、将AI批判扎根于文化知识与创造性实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨学生和教师如何通过参与式设计共同决定生成式AI工具在课堂中的使用方式，填补关于青年主导的AI教育决策研究的空白。

Method: 五周参与式设计项目，包括三名11年级拉丁裔学生和三名高中教师；通过视频录像和设计制品进行质性分析。

Result: 识别出三种关键AI素养实践：集体质疑AI假设、互补专长促成相互学习、将AI批判融入文化知识与创造性实践。

Conclusion: 参与式设计方法能让学生塑造AI工具在课堂中的采纳与批判性审视，拓展了"青年作为主角"的研究路线，为围绕新兴技术培养批判性AI素养提供了设计策略。

Abstract: Who gets to decide how generative AI tools enter students' classrooms? We report on a five-week participatory design program in which three 11th-grade Latinx students and three high school teachers in California negotiated how generative AI tools would be used and taught about in learning environments. Drawing on video recordings and designed artifacts, we ask: what critical AI literacy practices emerged as students and teachers jointly designed how generative AI tools would be used and taught about? Our analysis reveals three practices: collectively unsettling assumptions about AI, mutual learning through complementary expertise, and grounding AI critique in cultural knowledge and creative practice. Students and teachers developed these practices through the design work itself. This case contributes strategies for designing with youth around an emergent technology like generative AI toward critical AI literacy. It extends work on youth as protagonists by showing how this approach enables students to shape both the adoption and the interrogation of these tools in their learning environments.

</details>


### [126] [Spontaneous Persuasion: An Audit of Model Persuasiveness in Everyday Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22109)
*Nalin Poungpeth,Nicholas Clark,Tanu Mitra*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在多轮对话中表现出显著的"自发说服"现象，即在用户寻求信息或建议的非劝说场景中，模型仍系统性地使用说服策略。研究发现LLMs在几乎所有对话中都会自发说服用户，主要依赖基于信息的策略（如逻辑推理和量化证据），而人类回应则更倾向于社会影响策略（如负面情感诉求和非专业证词）。这种差异可能解释了LLMs为何能有效说服用户，以及为何被视为客观公正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将说服力定义为在对抗性场景中有意制造最有说服力的论点，但这无法捕捉日常人机交互中用户寻求信息或建议的真实场景。为了弥补这一空白，作者引入"自发说服"概念，描述在日常对话中非明确使用说服策略的情况。

Method: 对五种LLMs进行审计，通过多轮对话揭示自发说服的出现频率和技术手段。基于心理学、传播学和语言学文献构建用户回应分类法以模拟回应风格。同时收集Reddit上人类对相同话题的回应进行对比分析。

Result: LLMs在几乎所有对话中都会自发说服用户，且高度依赖基于信息的策略（逻辑推理、量化证据）。该结果在不同模型和用户回应风格间保持一致。在心理健康相关对话中，评估型和情感型策略的使用率更高。相比之下，人类回应更倾向于使用社会影响力策略（负面情感诉求、非专业证词）。

Conclusion: LLMs和人类在自发说服策略上的分布差异，可能解释了LLMs说服用户的有效性以及用户对模型客观公正的感知。这一发现对理解人机交互中AI的潜在影响力具有重要启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) possess strong persuasive capabilities that outperform humans in head-to-head comparisons. Users report consulting LLMs to inform major life decisions in relationships, medical settings, and when seeking professional advice. Prior work measures persuasion as intentional attempts at producing the most effective argument or convincing statement. This fails to capture everyday human-AI interactions in which users seek information or advice. To address this gap, we introduce "spontaneous persuasion," which characterizes the inexplicit use of persuasive strategies in everyday scenarios where persuasion is not necessarily warranted. We conduct an audit of five LLMs to uncover how frequently and through which techniques spontaneous persuasion appears in multi-turn conversations. To simulate response styles, we provide a user response taxonomy grounded in literature from psychology, communication, and linguistics. Furthermore, we compare the distribution of spontaneous persuasion produced by LLMs with human responses on the same topics, collected from Reddit. We find LLMs spontaneously persuade the user in virtually all conversations, heavily relying on information-based strategies such as appeals to logic or quantitative evidence. This was consistent across models and user response styles, but conversations concerning mental health saw higher rates of appraisal-based and emotion-based strategies. In comparison, human responses tended to invoke strategies that generate social influence, like negative emotion appeals and non-expert testimony. This difference may explain the effectiveness of LLM in persuading users, as well as the perception of models as objective and impartial.

</details>


### [127] [Same Project, Different Start: How Contribution Events Shape Activity and Retention in Open Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22120)
*Mohamed Ouf,Mariam Guizani*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过匹配队列研究（2001名活动贡献者 vs 2001名有机贡献者），发现活动贡献者成为核心成员的概率更高（12.1% vs 9.6%）、留存时间更长（中位数8.2 vs 4.8月）。三种参与模式（稳态、前期集中、间歇性）对应不同留存效果，但导师制贡献者在失去项目支持后留存率下降，存在导师依赖效应。前12周的参与模式对长期轨迹有强预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源项目依赖长期贡献者，但大多数新人在单次贡献后就离开。虽然有Google Summer of Code、Hacktoberfest等活动每年吸引数千名新人，但这些活动是否真正产生持久贡献者尚不明确。

Method: 采用匹配队列研究设计，从330个项目中选取2001名活动参与贡献者和2001名有机贡献者进行对比，通过统计数据比较核心贡献者比例、留存时间及参与模式。

Result: 活动贡献者成为核心贡献者的几率显著更高（12.1% vs 9.6%, OR=1.31），留存时间更长（中位数8.2 vs 4.8月）。导师制贡献者68.9%保持稳定周活动，非导师制61.0%呈前期集中模式，有机贡献者57.0%呈间歇模式。稳定模式留存最长（中位数13月），但导师依赖效应导致支持丧失后留存下降。

Conclusion: 活动贡献机制能有效促进长期贡献，但不同类型活动产生根本不同的参与节奏和留存效果。导师制虽带来更稳定参与，但也产生依赖性。新人前12周的行为模式是长期留存的关键预测指标。

Abstract: Open source projects depend on newcomers who stay, yet most leave after a single contribution. Contribution events such as Google Summer of Code, LFX Mentorship, Hacktoberfest, and 24 Pull Requests attract thousands of newcomers each year, but whether they produce lasting contributors remains unclear. We conduct the first matched-cohort study comparing 2,001 event-based and 2,001 organic contributors across 330 projects. Our results reveal three key findings. First, event contributors have significantly higher odds of becoming core contributors (12.1% vs. 9.6%, p < 0.001, OR = 1.31) and stay significantly longer (median 8.2 vs. 4.8 months). Second, each entry mechanism is associated with a fundamentally different engagement rhythm: 68.9% of mentorship contributors sustain Steady weekly activity across their first 12 weeks, whereas 61.0% of non-mentorship contributors exhibit Front-Loading and 57.0% of organic contributors exhibit Intermittent engagement (p < 0.001). Third, Steady engagement is associated with significantly longer retention regardless of group (median 13 vs. 8 months for Front-Loading), yet mentorship contributors who lose their program scaffolding show shorter retention than self-sustained non-mentorship contributors, revealing a mentor-dependency effect. A newcomer's first 12 weeks are strongly indicative of their long-term trajectory.

</details>


### [128] [ArguMath: AI-Simulated Environment for Pre-Service Teacher Training in Orchestrating Classroom Mathematics Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22205)
*Jiwon Chun,Yuling Zhuang,Armanto Sutedjo,Colin Xu,Rong Ren,Meng Xia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出ArguMath——一个基于大语言模型的AI模拟课堂环境，帮助职前数学教师在低风险环境中练习数学论证的课堂组织技能。通过八位经验教师的形成性研究确定设计需求，七位职前教师的探索性研究表明该系统能有效提升其理论导向的提问策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职前数学教师在实践中运用抽象理论知识、尤其是提出理性问题的能力有限，且缺乏低风险的真实教学练习机会。

Method: 通过八位经验教师的形成性研究确定设计需求（个性化、真实模拟、结构化反思、易用性），开发包含课堂自定义、基于真实语料的AI学生模拟与实时教学建议、话语标注与反馈三大模块的ArguMath系统，并对七位职前教师进行探索性用户研究及四位经验教师访谈。

Result: ArguMath能够支持职前教师提升课堂组织技能，特别是在符合理论的提问策略方面表现出潜力。

Conclusion: AI模拟课堂环境结合结构化反思可以有效弥补职前教师理论与实践之间的鸿沟，为数学论证教学提供低风险的实践平台。

Abstract: Facilitating productive mathematical argumentation, especially asking rational questions, is essential yet remains challenging for pre-service mathematics teachers (PMTs), who often have limited opportunities to apply abstract theoretical knowledge in authentic practice. At the same time, recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have expanded the potential for simulating students in educational settings, enabling low-risk environments for instructional practice. To inform the design of a system that supports PMTs in orchestrating classroom argumentation, we conducted a formative study with eight experienced mathematics teachers to identify key design requirements, including personalization, realistic simulations, structured reflection, and ease of use. Building on these requirements, we developed ArguMath, an AI-simulated classroom environment that supports PMTs in practicing the orchestration of mathematical argumentation. ArguMath comprises three core components: (1) customization of classroom settings; (2) simulation of classroom discussions with AI-based students grounded in authentic transcripts and augmented with real-time instructional suggestions; and (3) structured reflection through discourse annotation and overall feedback. Results from an exploratory user study with seven PMTs, complemented by interviews with four experienced teachers, indicate that ArguMath has the potential to support PMTs' classroom orchestration skills, particularly theory-aligned questioning strategies.

</details>


### [129] [Multi-Agent Consensus as a Cognitive Bias Trigger in Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22277)
*Soohwan Lee,Kyungho Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 多智能体AI系统中，智能体间的共识/异议结构（而非内容本身）会作为偏见信号影响人类判断：多数共识加速观点转变并提升信心，少数异议则促进深思熟虑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多智能体AI系统日益普及，用户面对的不再是单一AI声音而是集体声音，这种转变引入的社会动态（共识、异议、渐进趋同）可能触发认知偏见并扭曲人类判断，亟需系统性研究。

Method: 进行受控实验（N=127），比较三种多智能体配置（多数、少数、扩散），结合定量分析（观点变化、信心水平）和定性分析（用户解释轨迹）。

Result: 定量结果：多数共识加速观点改变并膨胀信心（符合社会证明和从众启发式）；少数异议减缓进程并促进深思熟虑。定性结果：识别出三种解释轨迹——强化、对齐、振荡，受用户对智能体独立性和群体动态的理解影响。

Conclusion: 智能体协议结构（独立于内容）在LLM交互中作为偏见相关信号起作用，研究为Bias4Trust议程提供了将多智能体社会影响作为可设计的偏见来源的理论基础。

Abstract: As multi-agent AI systems become more common, users increasingly encounter not a single AI voice but a collective one. This shift introduces social dynamics, such as consensus, dissent, and gradual convergence, that can trigger cognitive biases and distort human judgment. We present findings from a controlled experiment (N = 127) comparing three multi-agent configurations: Majority, Minority, and Diffusion. Quantitative results show that majority consensus accelerates opinion change and inflates confidence, consistent with social proof and bandwagon heuristics. Minority dissent slows this process and promotes more deliberative engagement. Qualitative analysis identifies three interpretive trajectories: reinforcing, aligning, and oscillating, shaped by how users interpret agent independence and group dynamics over time. These findings suggest that agent agreement structure, independent of content, functions as a bias-relevant signal in LLM interactions. We hope this work contributes to the Bias4Trust agenda by grounding multi-agent social influence as a concrete and designable source of bias in human-AI interaction.

</details>


### [130] [Large Language Model Counterarguments in Older Adults: Cognitive Offloading or Vulnerability to Moral Persuasion?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22356)
*Kou Tamura,Sayaka Ishibashi,Ayana Goma,Kenta Yamamoto,Kouhei Masumoto*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 大型语言模型(LLMs)生成的反对论点能影响年轻人和老年人的道德判断，超过30%的参与者在两种电车困境中改变了判断。老年人更容易被说服，尤其是在认知功能较低的群体中，而信任和先前经验并不能预测判断改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLMs生成的反论据是否影响不同年龄段人群的道德判断，以及这种影响如何因困境类型、认知功能、对AI的信任和LLM使用经验而异。

Method: 130名参与者（56名年轻人，74名老年人）面对开关型和人行桥型电车困境，收到ChatGPT生成的、反对其初始判断的论点，测量判断变化及相关变量。

Result: 超过30%的参与者在两种困境中改变了道德判断（开关困境32.31%，人行桥困境36.92%）。老年人比年轻人更可能改变判断，在开关困境中改变程度更大。在情感厌恶的人行桥困境中，认知功能较低的老年人更倾向于与LLM论点一致。对AI的信任和先前使用经验不能预测判断逆转。

Conclusion: LLMs可以作为认知卸载工具补偿年龄相关的认知下降，但也对认知脆弱个体构成被过度说服的风险。信任与说服之间存在脱节。

Abstract: This study examined whether counterarguments generated by large language models (LLMs) influence the moral judgments of younger and older adults and whether these effects vary as a function of dilemma type, cognitive functioning, trust in AI, and prior experience using LLMs. Using the switch and footbridge trolley dilemmas, 130 participants (56 younger adults and 74 older adults) were presented with ChatGPT arguments that opposed their initial judgments. Results revealed that more than 30% of participants reversed their moral judgments in both dilemmas (32.31% in the switch dilemma and 36.92% in the footbridge dilemma), suggesting that LLMs possess substantial persuasive power. Older adults tended to be more likely than younger adults to reverse their judgments, and they showed a significantly greater degree of judgment change in the switch dilemma. Notably, in the emotionally aversive footbridge dilemma, older adults with lower cognitive functioning were significantly more likely to align with the LLM-generated counterargument. General trust in AI and prior experience with LLMs did not predict judgment reversal, supporting a disconnect between trust and persuasion. Instead, individual factors such as lower initial confidence and higher perceived task difficulty were associated with greater susceptibility to AI influence. These findings suggest that, although LLMs may serve as tools for cognitive offloading that compensate for age-related cognitive decline, they may also pose a risk of undue persuasion for cognitively vulnerable individuals.

</details>


### [131] [Point & Grasp: Flexible Selection of Out-of-Reach Objects Through Probabilistic Cue Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22491)
*Xuejing Luo,Hee-Seung Moon,Christian Holz,Antti Oulasvirta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种概率性线索融合框架，用于混合现实中远距离物体选择。通过结合指向方向与抓取手势（Point&Grasp），并收集ORG数据集训练鲁棒的抓取似然模型，用户研究表明该方法在准确率和速度上优于单一线索基线，且在多种模糊场景下与最先进方法相比保持实用效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合现实中远距离物体选择方法依赖单一线索或确定性融合多种线索，当主导线索不可靠时性能下降，亟需一种灵活的概率性线索融合方法。

Method: 提出概率性线索融合框架，结合指向方向与抓取手势（Point&Grasp）；收集ORG数据集训练抓取手势的似然模型。

Result: 用户研究表明该方法在准确率和速度上优于单一线索基线，并在多种模糊场景下与最先进方法相比仍保持实用效果。

Conclusion: 概率性线索融合框架能有效提升混合现实中远距离物体选择的鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: Selecting out-of-reach objects is a fundamental task in mixed reality (MR). Existing methods rely on a single cue or deterministically fuse multiple cues, leading to performance degradation when the dominant cue becomes unreliable. In this work, we introduce a probabilistic cue integration framework that enables flexible combination of multiple user-generated cues for intent inference. Inspired by natural grasping behavior, we instantiate the framework with pointing direction and grasp gestures as a new interaction technique, Point&Grasp. To this end, we collect the Out-of-Reach Grasping (ORG) dataset to train a robust likelihood model of the gestural cue, which captures grasping patterns not present in existing in-reach datasets. User studies demonstrate that our selection method with cue integration not only improves accuracy and speed over single-cue baselines, but also remains practically effective compared to state-of-the-art methods across various sources of ambiguity. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/drlxj/point-and-grasp.

</details>


### [132] [Catheter Monitoring in Intelligent Endovascular Navigation Systems: Interactive Simulations and Mixed Reality for Enhanced Navigational Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22497)
*Veronica Ruozzi,Giovanni Battista Regazzo,Maria Chiara Palumbo,Wim-Alexander Beckers,Mouloud Ourak,Xiu Zhang,Francesca Perico,Alessandro Caimi,Emmanuel Vander Poorten,Emiliano Votta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文开发并测试了一个集成实时导管形状重建、交互式有限元模拟与混合现实可视化的框架，用于在血管内导航中监测导管-血管相互作用。实验表明，该系统能够以可接受的精度（中位位移误差<2.33 mm）实时渲染血管变形，尽管在迂曲部位计算时间超出实际时间45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在血管内介入导航过程中，实时监测导管与血管壁之间的相互作用对于提高手术安全性和操作精度至关重要。现有方法缺乏将实时传感数据与生物力学模拟及可视化有效集成的框架。

Method: 基于CT数据构建从右股静脉到下腔静脉的有限元模型；采用光纤布拉格光栅和电磁传感器实现导管实时形状感知；通过拉格朗日乘子法建模导管-血管接触；在硅胶血管复制品上进行体外验证；通过立体帧三角测量获取地面真值进行精度评估。

Result: 初始导航阶段模拟时间超出实际时间12%，导管到达迂曲部分时超出45%；Hololens 2渲染稳定在35-40 FPS；两阶段血管壁位移中位相对误差分别小于1 mm和2.33 mm。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将交互式生物力学模拟与实时传感器数据集成以持续监测导管-血管相互作用的可行性，混合现实可视化可作为支持操作者决策的用户界面。

Abstract: Purpose: Developing and testing a framework that integrates real-time catheter shape reconstruction, interactive simulations, and mixed reality visualization to enable accurate monitoring of catheter-vessel interactions during endovascular navigation.
  Methods: A finite element model (FEM) of the venous pathway from the right femoral vein to the inferior vena cava was generated from computed tomography data and implemented into an interactive simulation. Catheter motion was imposed as boundary condition, and catheter-vessel contact was modeled with a Lagrange multiplier formulation to compute vessel deformation. The framework was tested in-vitro using a sensorized catheter with Fiber Bragg Grating and electromagnetic sensors as it was advanced through a silicone replica of the vascular anatomy. Real-time sensor read-outs fed the simulation, and the updated catheter and vessel geometries were streamed to Hololens 2. The performance and accuracy of FEM-computed vessel wall displacement were validated against experimental ground-truth obtained via stereo frames triangulation.
  Results: The simulated time exceeded the real temporal extent by 12% during initial navigation and by 45% when the catheter reached the most tortuous portion. Hololens 2 rendering remained stable at 35-40 frames per second. The median relative displacement error between FEM-computed and ground-truth vessel wall displacements remained below 1 mm and 2.33 mm for these two phases, respectively.
  Conclusion: The study demonstrates the feasibility of integrating interactive biomechanical simulation with real-time sensor data to enable continuous monitoring of catheter-vessel interactions, with mixed reality visualization serving as a user interface to support operator decision-making.

</details>


### [133] [How GenAI is Helping Reimagine Antenatal Care in A Low-Resource Setting: From Provider Enablement to Patient Empowerment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22610)
*Maryam Mustafa,Imaan Hameed,Amna Shahnawaz,Bilal A Mateen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Awaaz-e-Sehat——一个基于语音的AI系统，通过在巴基斯坦三年迭代开发，从面向医生的语音转EMR工具演变为以患者为中心的WhatsApp平台，让孕妇自主生成结构化临床笔记、接收AI产前指导并通过二维码共享医疗记录，重新定义了产前护理模式，将患者置于健康数据生成和拥有的核心位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴基斯坦孕产妇死亡率居高不下（2023年每10万活产155例），主要因为纸质记录碎片化、识字率低、优质医疗可及性差以及性别障碍导致的护理连续性中断，亟需创新的数字健康解决方案。

Method: 经过三年迭代设计、部署和开发，系统从面向临床医生的Urdu语音转EMR工具演变为基于WhatsApp的患者中心平台，支持患者自主生成结构化临床笔记、获取AI产前指导，并通过二维码在全国范围内与医疗提供者共享记录。

Result: 系统成功转型为患者主导的健康数据生成和共享平台，将EMR和临床决策支持系统从静态机构工具转变为动态的自我倡导和共同责任工具。

Conclusion: 在临床医生时间有限且缺乏机构激励的环境中，Awaaz-e-Sehat提出了一种以患者为中心的护理模式，让患者积极参与生成和拥有自身健康数据，通过让患者了解自身风险因素并将其纳入临床决策支持循环，重新定义了产前护理和电子医疗记录本身。

Abstract: Despite steady global advances, maternal mortality remains alarmingly high in Pakistan (155 deaths per 100,000 live births in 2023); largely as a consequence of fragmented paper records, low literacy, poor access to quality healthcare, and gendered barriers that compromise care continuity. Over three years, we designed, deployed, and iteratively developed Awaaz-e-Sehat, a speech-based artificial intelligence (AI) system that generates electronic medical records (EMRs) and supports decision-making in maternal health. The tool evolved from a clinician-facing AI assistant that automated Urdu speech-to-EMR generation into a patient-centred WhatsApp-based platform, enabling women to generate their own structured clinical notes, receive AI-generated antenatal guidance, and share QR-coded records with providers anywhere in the country. This case study documents that translational journey, i.e., how the ground realities of workload, linguistic nuance, and infrastructural constraints reshaped our design. The result is not merely a new method of record-keeping, but a reimagining of antenatal care and electronic medical records themselves. In settings where clinicians are time-constrained and have little institutional incentive to document, Awaaz-e-Sehat proposes a model of care that centres patients as active participants in generating and owning their health data. By keeping patients informed about their own risk factors and integrating them into the clinical decision-support loop, the system transforms EMRs and CDSS from static institutional artefacts into dynamic tools for self-advocacy and shared accountability in maternal health.

</details>


### [134] [What People See (and Miss) About Generative AI Risks: Perceptions of Failures, Risks, and Who Should Address Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22654)
*Megan Li,Wendy Bickersteth,Ningjing Tang,Parv Kapoor,Khinezin Win,Peter Zhong,Jason I. Hong,Lorrie Faith Cranor,Hoda Heidari,Hong Shen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文开发并验证了一套调查工具，基于真实事件和故障模式分类法，评估公众对生成式AI故障模式、风险及责任方认知。研究发现该工具有效反映公众实际使用场景中的风险感知，可为AI素养教育和治理提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式AI风险日益受到关注，但公众对这些风险及其背后的故障模式（即在GenAI生命周期中反复出现的社会技术失效模式）的认知尚不清楚，缺乏有效的评估工具。

Method: 设计调查问卷，经8位领域专家验证，在960名美国参与者样本中实施。问卷以公开报道的真实事件为场景，基于GenAI故障模式分类法构建，评估公众对故障模式、风险及利益相关方责任的认知。

Result: 该工具(1)能有效评估公众在真实使用场景中的风险感知，且可扩展至未来的新场景；(2)对设计AI素养教育工具和干预措施具有潜在实用价值。

Conclusion: AI素养教育和治理策略应贴近人们在日常生活中的真实接触和推理方式。

Abstract: Despite growing concerns about the risks of Generative AI (GenAI), there is limited understanding of public perceptions of these risks and their associated failure modes -- defined as recurring patterns of sociotechnical breakdown across the GenAI lifecycle that contribute to risks of real-world harm. To address this gap, we present a survey instrument, validated with eight subject matter experts and deployed on a sample of 960 U.S.-based participants, to assess awareness and perceptions of GenAI's failure modes, their associated risks, and stakeholder responsibilities to address them. To support realism and content validity, our instrument is structured around scenarios grounded in publicly reported incidents and a taxonomy of GenAI's failure modes. Findings suggest that our instrument is (1) effective for assessing risk awareness and perceptions in a way that is grounded in people's current contexts of use, yet is extensible to new contexts that will inevitably arise; and (2) potentially useful for informing the design of AI literacy tools and interventions. We argue for AI literacy and governance approaches that align with how people encounter and reason about GenAI in everyday life.

</details>
